 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# STACK: a certified canonical-KV pushdown interpreter on Savant r3
     2	
     3	Brief: `STACK_BRIEF.md`. Referee verdict: `GATE.md` C1 (NARROW). The surviving claim is a practical, certified
     4	canonical-KV implementation of a visibly pushdown interpreter on a pretrained LLM. We do not claim that finite
     5	recursive verification is new (Cai, Shin & Song 2017).
     6	
     7	## Design
     8	
     9	**Machines** (`stack_machine.py`, no torch). These are visibly pushdown tables. The input symbol alone decides push,
    10	pop or local. The host owns the stack. The model is asked only for the next control state of a row
    11	(q, top, a). The stack operation is a pure host function of that same row.
    12	
    13	- **Dyck-k** (k = 2, 4).
    14	  - States OK, BAD and the sinks HA (accept) and HR (reject).
    15	  - Frames BOT, F0 … F(k−1).
    16	  - Alphabet O*i*, C*i*, END.
    17	- **Boolean evaluator** (and, or, not, with parentheses, in prefix form).
    18	  - `AND`, `OR`, `NOT` push a frame. `TT`, `FF` are literals. `RP` closes. `END` halts.
    19	  - States Z (expecting an operand), T, F, E (error), and the sinks HT, HF, HE.
    20	  - Frames BOT, AND0, ANDT, ANDF, OR0, ORT, ORF, NOT. A binary frame stores its left operand once it is known.
    21	  - The BBH surface syntax (infix, `not` binds tighter than `and`, which binds tighter than `or`) is compiled to
    22	    prefix tokens by Python's own `ast` parser. This compiler runs on the host, not in the model. The model
    23	    executes the compiled program.
    24	
    25	**Return-value transfer.** A pop row's next state is combine(child state, popped frame). It is not a restored
    26	parent state. The two child states of every return group lead to the targets below. The table is
    27	`return_transfer` in the audit.
    28	
    29	| Return group | Child state → next state |
    30	| --- | --- |
    31	| NOT | T→F, F→T |
    32	| ANDT | T→T, F→F |
    33	| ANDF | T→F, F→F |
    34	| ORF | T→T, F→F |
    35	| ORT | T→T, F→T |
    36	| Dyck, matching close in OK | stays OK |
    37	| Dyck, matching close in BAD | stays BAD (a failed child is not rewritten to OK) |
    38	
    39	**Bottom, malformed and halting rows.** All are rows of the same table, and all are certified.
    40	
    41	- *Bottom rows:* a return while the top is BOT. The stack stays and the state becomes the error state.
    42	- *Malformed rows:* every other transition into E, BAD or HR.
    43	- *Halting rows:*
    44	  - END enters a sink.
    45	  - A sink maps every symbol to itself and freezes the stack.
    46	
    47	**Certified rows.** These are all rows witnessed by a concrete stack. Stability between stack limits 6 and 10 is
    48	only a diagnostic, not a completeness proof. `stack_validate.closed_cover` independently checks an inductive upper
    49	bound: Dyck permits OK/BAD/HR with any frame and HA only with BOT; Boolean permits Z/T/F/E/HE with any frame and
    50	HT/HF only with BOT. Initialization is in the set. For each pair and event, the check considers every possible
    51	parent exposed by a pop and verifies that the successor pair stays in the set and BOT is preserved. Deeper
    52	ancestors cannot affect this transition. Every pair in this closed upper bound also has a concrete witness from
    53	the search, so the witnessed rows are complete at arbitrary depth. The offline audit checks 110, 576 and 2,009
    54	pair/event/parent cases respectively, without changing the tables used by the recorded GPU jobs.
    55	
    56	| Machine | Rows | Bottom | Malformed | Halt or halt entry | Call | Internal | Return |
    57	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
    58	| Dyck-2 | 50 | 4 | 8 | 26 | 6 | – | 6 |
    59	| Dyck-4 | 144 | 8 | 32 | 64 | 20 | – | 20 |
    60	| Boolean | 294 | 4 | 118 | 102 | 36 | 24 | 10 |
    61	
    62	**Π.** Π is the table, one row per line: ` top q a q2\n`. Every symbol is one token with one leading space, taken
    63	from the pin's name pool (`pb_pin.name_pool`, test split). Π is prefilled once. Its KV is kept, and every step
    64	works on a clone, so Π's cache is never written.
    65	
    66	**One step (canonical construction).**
    67	- The step is one forward of the 3-token block [top][q][a] at the restarted positions |Π|, |Π|+1, |Π|+2, over a clone
    68	  of Π's cache. It is the pinned tick (`pb_pin` / `scan_forward.single_lookup`) with a 3-token block.
    69	- The next state is the argmax over the full vocabulary at the event position.
    70	- The block is then discarded.
    71	- The host applies the row's stack operation.
    72	- The stack is a list of frame symbols outside attention.
    73	
    74	**The top frame's KV** is the KV of the token `top` at position |Π|. It is recomputed at every step from (Π, top).
    75	
    76	**Naive construction (the comparison the referee asked for).** The frame's original KV is kept:
    77	- the stack is a KV cache Π ⊕ frames;
    78	- a push runs the frame token over Π and all the frames below it;
    79	- a pop crops.
    80	
    81	A step then shows Π, that top frame's stored KV, and [q][a]. There are two variants:
    82	- **B:** every frame is at the restarted position |Π|. History enters only through attention.
    83	- **A:** the frame is at its stack position |Π| + depth, and [q][a] follow it.
    84	
    85	Both are compared with the control: the same 2-token step over Π ⊕ the canonical template KV of that frame symbol.
    86	
    87	## The invariant and its proof sketch
    88	
    89	**Invariant (canonical frames).** In the canonical construction, the forward of every step is a function of
    90	(Π, top, q, a) alone:
    91	- the token ids are those three symbols;
    92	- the positions are the fixed |Π|, |Π|+1, |Π|+2;
    93	- the cache is a clone of Π's prefill, and no step writes it;
    94	- nothing else enters the forward. The stack below the top, the depth, and earlier operations are not visible,
    95	  in tokens, positions or cache.
    96	
    97	So the top frame's KV (slot |Π|, all layers) and the step's logits are deterministic functions of the row. On one
    98	host and one code path (same shapes, same kernels) they are bitwise equal to the logits recorded when that row was
    99	certified.
   100	
   101	**Theorem sketch.** Suppose every reachable row's certified argmax is its target, with a positive margin. Then by
   102	induction on the step count:
   103	1. The model state equals the reference VPA state after every step, for every input of every length and depth.
   104	2. The host stack therefore equals the reference stack, because the operation is the same pure function of the same
   105	   row.
   106	3. The next step addresses a reachable row. By the invariant, its logits are that row's certified logits.
   107	
   108	Depth is unbounded because no step reads it. The proof needs no check per depth: the certificate covers the finite
   109	set of rows, and the invariant covers every realizable cache.
   110	
   111	The empirical part is the bitwise claim. It is checked at every step of every counted run:
   112	- the step logits are compared with the certified row;
   113	- the frame KV is compared with the frame symbol's template.
   114	
   115	If any step is not bitwise equal, the fallback diagnostic is the margin rule: every row's margin must exceed
   116	2·max |Δlogit|. A maximum measured across sampled steps certifies only those perturbations; an all-execution
   117	robustness claim needs a uniform bound. The unbounded-depth argument instead uses the fixed-view invariant and
   118	assumes deterministic inference on the certified path.
   119	
   120	The call/return partition fixes stack height changes before a halt, except returns at BOT. Boolean internal
   121	events may rewrite the top symbol, and a call may store a left operand in the parent before pushing. Thus this
   122	is a visibly driven pushdown interpreter with top rewrites, rather than the strict textbook VPA syntax in which
   123	internal transitions leave the stack untouched. Halted machines freeze on all symbols. These conventions are
   124	part of the host semantics and the certificate, including malformed inputs.
   125	
   126	## Registered predictions
   127	
   128	These were registered and committed before any GPU spend. The cap is $20 (brief). The ledger is
   129	`results/modal_ledger.json`, app `polyformer-stack`, volume `polyformer-stack-results`.
   130	
   131	**Inputs.**
   132	- Seed 20261029, `stack_machine.CELLS`. Each cell ends in END.
   133	- Depth cells d8, d64, d512 and d4096 reach exactly that stack depth.
   134	- L20000 has 20,000 symbols:
   135	  - Dyck reaches depth 512;
   136	  - Boolean is a not-spine of 64 around an and/or tree, depth 78.
   137	- Random instances (`stack_machine.instances`): 8 per machine at depths 8, 64 and 512.
   138	  - Dyck alternates well-formed and malformed (one close bracket's type swapped).
   139	  - Boolean chains mix NOT, nested-left and nested-right operands.
   140	- The malformed cells, and BBH boolean_expressions (official 250, BBH commit 9ee07bd).
   141	
   142	**Predictions.**
   143	
   144	1. **Canonical frames (kill test 1).**
   145	   - In the canonical construction, every frame symbol's KV at slot |Π| is bitwise equal to its template at every
   146	     step of every counted run, at every depth (up to 4,096) and history.
   147	   - Every real step's logits are bitwise equal to the certified row's.
   148	   - Under the naive construction, the frame KV differs from the template (max |ΔK| > 0) at every stack depth ≥ 1,
   149	     in both variants.
   150	   - Naive variant A makes step errors (argmax ≠ the row's target) at depth 512 on at least one machine.
   151	   - Variant B's error rate is at most variant A's.
   152	
   153	2. **Certificate (kill test 2).**
   154	   - Zero-shot Savant r3 with the first symbol assignment certifies all 50 Dyck-2 rows.
   155	   - The Boolean evaluator (294 rows) has at least one miss zero-shot.
   156	   - After the registered remedies, all three machines certify every reachable row with min margin > 0. The
   157	     remedies, in order:
   158	     1. symbol assignments 0–15, the first that certifies;
   159	     2. then a LoRA of at most 300 steps on stack-shaped random tables over the train name pool, which is
   160	        disjoint from the evaluated symbols.
   161	
   162	3. **Runs (kill test 3).**
   163	   - The certified executor is exact on all 15 counted cells. Exact means:
   164	     - every step's state equals the reference;
   165	     - the final verdict is correct;
   166	     - the stack never pops BOT.
   167	   - It is also exact on all 72 random instances, on every malformed input (each ends in the right sink), and on
   168	     BBH boolean_expressions 250/250.
   169	
   170	4. **Comparators.**
   171	   - **Plain decoding with history, same model.** Π, then the transcript of rows; the host supplies the top
   172	     frame, and positions are not restarted. It is not exact on at least one machine at depth 64. Its exact rate
   173	     at depth 512 is below 50% on every machine. Cells whose transcript exceeds the 40,960-token context are
   174	     reported as not runnable.
   175	   - **Base Qwen3-4B with thinking** (natural-language prompt, temperature 0.6, budget to a 32,768-token context):
   176	     - at depth 8, at least 75% correct;
   177	     - at depth 512, below 75% on every machine.
   178	   - **BBH boolean official 250.** The certified executor gets 250/250. Cited comparisons, not re-run: base
   179	     thinking 99 and base answer mode 97.6% (CLOSEOUT §6.4); Savant r2 writing its own programs, 22.0%.
   180	
   181	5. **Speed.** These are steps of the canonical executor, ms per step, mean over the counted cells' steps:
   182	   - under 50 ms on an H100;
   183	   - under 150 ms on an L4.
   184	
   185	**Kill outcome.** STACK is killed if either holds:
   186	- no certificate for the Boolean evaluator after the remedies of prediction 2, the last being a LoRA of at most 300
   187	  steps;
   188	- canonical frames cannot be made history-independent. That means a canonical step's frame KV or logits differ
   189	  across depth or history, and the margin rule does not cover the difference.
   190	
   191	## Results
   192	
   193	**Kill outcome: survives on the measured H100 execution path.** All 488 reachable rows certify with positive margins, and every counted canonical step reproduces its certified logits and frame KV bitwise. The Boolean zero-shot-failure prediction was wrong: no assignment search or LoRA was needed. This supports the narrowed C1 implementation claim; it does not establish a new verification theorem.
   194	
   195	Written by `stack_report.py` from `results/`. The host for counted runs is NVIDIA H100 80GB HBM3 unless a row says otherwise. Model: Qwen/Qwen3-4B with Savant r3 (`arith-s1r2/adapter_step500`) merged, bf16, SDPA. No LoRA was trained: every machine certified zero-shot at symbol assignment 0, so the fallback in `stack_lora.py` was never run.

   244	
   245	Within certification, a frame symbol's slot-|Π| KV is bitwise equal across every row with that top. It is **not** bitwise equal to a 1-token forward of the same symbol (max |Δ| up to 0.5). This is consistent with numerical differences between the 3-token and 1-token execution shapes; the experiment does not isolate the responsible kernel. The invariant is stated for one code path, and the executor uses only the 3-token path, so this does not enter the certificate. The naive comparison below uses 1-token pushes and the 1-token template, so its depth-0 row is like with like.
   246	
   247	### Naive construction: keep each frame's original KV
   248	
   249	The trajectory is the reference one, so errors do not compound. Each row aggregates sampled steps. **Control** means the same 2-token step over Π ⊕ the canonical 1-token template. **Rel** is ‖KV_naive − KV_template‖ / ‖KV_template‖ over all layers.

   283	
   284	**Depth-0 numerical control:** Dyck-4's naive path is not always bitwise equal even at BOT: 25/41 matches for B and 18/41 for A, with max abs ΔK 0.125 and ΔV 0.15625. All these depth-0 argmaxes remain correct, and the maximum logit difference is 0.25. The cause of this small drift was not isolated, so not every nonzero naive difference can be attributed solely to history. Nonbottom differences are much larger and include wrong states, while the separate three-token canonical path is bitwise on every counted step.

   318	### Comparators
   319	
   320	**Plain decoding with history, same model.** Π, then the transcript ` top q a q2\n`, positions not restarted. The host supplies the top frame; q is the model's own previous output. A run stops at its first wrong state.
   321	
   322	| machine | d8 exact | d64 exact | d512 exact | median first error at d512 (step) | counted cells d8 / d64 / d512 | d4096, L20000 |
   323	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
   324	| Dyck-2 | 8/8 | 8/8 | 7/8 | 516 | exact / exact / exact | not runnable (41215 tokens); not runnable (100255 tokens) |
   325	| Dyck-4 | 8/8 | 8/8 | 8/8 | – | exact / exact / exact | not runnable (41685 tokens); not runnable (100725 tokens) |
   326	| Boolean | 8/8 | 8/8 | 8/8 | – | exact / exact / error at step 509 | not runnable (42470 tokens); not runnable (101475 tokens) |
   327	
   328	On BBH boolean 250 the history decoder is exact on 250/250.
   329	
   330	**Base Qwen3-4B with thinking** (vLLM, temperature 0.6, top-p 0.95, top-k 20, budget to a 32768-token context). The prompt is natural language: a parenthesized Python Boolean expression, or a bracket string with a balanced/not question. Generation wall time was 1006.3 s.

   335	| Dyck-4 | 4/8 | 3/8 | 4/8 | 6/27 | wrong / wrong / wrong |
   336	| Boolean | 8/8 | 2/8 | 6/8 | 3/27 | right / wrong / wrong |

   348	
   349	### Speed
   350	
   351	- **H100:** 24.02 ms per canonical step, weighted by steps over the 15 counted cells (90459 steps). This includes the bitwise checks of the logits and the frame.
   352	- **L4:** 63.97 ms per step over the d8, d64 and d512 cells of all three machines (5870 steps). Every step was exact: True. The L4 certified its own rows: Dyck-2 50/50, Dyck-4 144/144, Boolean 294/294. Bits are per host and code path, so an L4 deployment needs its own certificate, and it has one.
   353	
   354	### Registered predictions: outcomes
   355	
   356	- **Canonical history independence: holds on the counted path.** Every counted logit and frame check is bitwise, including depth 4,096. The shape-dependent 1-token template is not substituted into this path.
   357	- **Certificates:** first-assignment Dyck-2 success holds; predicted zero-shot Boolean failure misses. All three certify at assignment 0. The conditional assignment search and ≤300-step LoRA were unnecessary.
   358	- **Runs:** all 15 cells, 72 random instances, all malformed cases and official BBH 250 pass the offline audit, including trace correctness, intended depth and input hashes. Depth 4,096 has one registered cell per machine; the eight-instance random suites cover depths 8, 64 and 512.
   359	- **Dyck-2 naive:** nonbottom sampled KV bitwise matches 0/6104; A errors at depth ≥512: 4/8. Total B errors 19/3090, A errors 227/3090; B≤A prediction holds. The per-depth table reports sampled bucket maxima, not a bound for all possible naive caches.
   360	- **Dyck-4 naive:** nonbottom sampled KV bitwise matches 0/6098; A errors at depth ≥512: 2/19. Total B errors 294/3090, A errors 67/3090; B≤A prediction misses. The per-depth table reports sampled bucket maxima, not a bound for all possible naive caches.
   361	- **Boolean naive:** nonbottom sampled KV bitwise matches 0/4472; A errors at depth ≥512: 0/2. Total B errors 132/2270, A errors 77/2270; B≤A prediction misses. The per-depth table reports sampled bucket maxima, not a bound for all possible naive caches.
   362	- **Naive A fails at depth 512 on at least one machine:** holds (6 sampled errors across machines).
   363	- **History fails on at least one machine's d64 random suite:** misses (0/24 failed traces).
   364	- **Dyck-2 comparators:** history fails 0/8 at d64; d512 <50% exact prediction misses. Thinking d8 ≥75% prediction misses (5/8); d512 <75% prediction holds (0/8).
   365	- **Dyck-4 comparators:** history fails 0/8 at d64; d512 <50% exact prediction misses. Thinking d8 ≥75% prediction misses (4/8); d512 <75% prediction holds (4/8).
   366	- **Boolean comparators:** history fails 0/8 at d64; d512 <50% exact prediction misses. Thinking d8 ≥75% prediction holds (8/8); d512 <75% prediction misses (6/8).
   367	- **Speed:** H100 24.02 ms <50 ms prediction holds; L4 63.97 ms <150 ms prediction holds. These include auditing overhead and are not optimized kernel benchmarks.
   368	
   369	### Scope and limitations
   370	
   371	The host supplies the program, compiles BBH syntax, maintains the symbolic stack and applies its operations. The model selects the next control state by full-vocabulary argmax at each real step. This is execution with a supplied compiler/table, not autonomous program synthesis. The history comparator also receives the host stack; the thinking comparator answers in natural language. The thinking runs cover d8/d64/d512 only, not d4096 or L20000. History cells exceeding its context budget are explicitly not runnable. No memory/latency advantage over StackTrans, stack attention, or fresh calls is measured, and no browser runtime is benchmarked.
   372	
   373	Zero-shot here means no additional stack-specific training on top of Savant r3; the pre-existing Savant adapter is part of the tested model. Short history-decoder successes are reported as successes; the result does not imply that canonical execution beats history on every input.
   374	
   375	The proof is conditional on deterministic execution of the fixed inference path and on the trusted host machine. Sampled bitwise checks support that premise; they do not prove arbitrary hardware determinism. A maximum logit deviation observed on test runs is an empirical tolerance, not a uniform bound on untested numerical perturbations. Model, dtype, quantization, runtime, GPU, batching, position policy or cache-layout changes require a new certificate.
   376

   392	
   393	## Browser port: exact implementation and acceptance steps
   394	
   395	This is a port specification; no browser implementation or browser speed claim is included in this experiment.
   396	
   397	1. **Freeze the artifact.** Merge `arith-s1r2/adapter_step500` into `Qwen/Qwen3-4B` using
   398	   `scan_forward.load_merged` / `pb_eval.merge_adapter`. Export those merged weights, configuration and tokenizer
   399	   to a browser GPU backend that exposes explicit per-layer past K/V, position IDs and attention masks. Record
   400	   file hashes, precision/quantization, backend version and GPU identity. A backend that only offers a chat or
   401	   generation API is insufficient. The current bf16 model needs a substantial device-memory budget; any smaller
   402	   quantized export is a new model configuration and must pass its own certificate.
   403	2. **Export the machine, not natural-language prompts.** Serialize `stack_machine.row_records` in its sorted
   404	   order and `stack_forward.machine_spec` with assignment 0: role→token IDs, newline ID, Π, start state, BOT,
   405	   halting states and each row's stack operation. Use the saved `cert.json` IDs to check the export. Each Π row
   406	   is exactly `[top, q, a, q2, newline]`; P is 250/720/1,470 for Dyck-2/Dyck-4/Boolean. No chat template, BOS,
   407	   padding or tokenizer-added special tokens may enter these integer sequences.
   408	3. **Port the trusted host machine.** Use an array of integer frame symbols initialized to BOT and one control
   409	   state. Translate `apply` operations `stay`, `rewrite`, `push`, `pop`, `store_push`; prohibit popping BOT.
   410	   Preserve child-result transfer in `bool_delta`. Port the closed-cover check and reference tests before GPU
   411	   inference. For infix Boolean text, use an iterative parser with `not > and > or` precedence, accepting only
   412	   Boolean literals/operators/parentheses, then emit the same prefix tokens and final END. Reject unknown tokens
   413	   explicitly. Validate the parser on all 250 pinned BBH inputs and the random-expression tests.
   414	4. **Prefill once.** Run Π at positions 0…P−1 with a causal mask; retain its per-layer K/V as immutable buffers.
   415	   Disable dropout and sampling. Allocate separate scratch for exactly three query tokens. The physical cache
   416	   indices and rotary positions of those tokens are P, P+1, P+2. Queries may attend to all Π and their own causal
   417	   prefix of the three-token block, never the external stack or an earlier step's scratch. Reuse buffers only
   418	   when the runtime guarantees the immutable Π prefix is not overwritten.
   419	5. **Implement the same three-token step.** Feed `[top_id, state_id, event_id]` as one block over Π. Use the
   420	   full-vocabulary argmax at the event position as the next state. Apply the host operation for the *incoming*
   421	   `(q, top, event)` row; do not replace the model's output with the reference target. Discard all three scratch
   422	   slots. Do not split the top token into a one-token prefill: that is a different numerical path. The step's
   423	   frame KV is scratch slot P across every layer, and the stack stores symbols, never those vectors.
   424	6. **Certify on the actual browser device.** Evaluate all 50/144/294 rows with the same batch size, shapes,
   425	   precision, mask and cache path as deployment. Require every full-vocabulary winner to equal its target with
   426	   strictly positive winner–runner-up margin. Retain logits per row and a slot-P frame per symbol; compare frames
   427	   across all rows sharing that symbol. A desktop H100/L4 certificate cannot authorize a browser export. Stop
   428	   on any failure; do not silently use a host lookup of the target state.
   429	7. **Run the port acceptance suite.** Replay the registered cells (depths 8/64/512/4,096 and L20000), all 72 random
   430	   instances, malformed inputs and BBH 250. Check each state against the reference and each full logit vector
   431	   and frame against the browser certificate, as `run_canonical` does. Hash Π before/after runs to catch cache
   432	   mutation. Repeat after changing depth/history and input order. If bitwise equality fails, report the observed
   433	   deviations and recertify the actual path; do not turn a sampled maximum into a universal bound.
   434	8. **Measure and retain provenance.** Run inference in a Web Worker, keep Π on the GPU, and yield progress between
   435	   short chunks of steps. Measure synchronized wall milliseconds per step and peak device memory separately
   436	   with and without auditing overhead. Log artifact hashes, certificate margins, device/runtime versions and
   437	   input hashes. Re-certify after any configuration change. Attention cache size is fixed by P+3; host stack
   438	   storage grows with nesting depth. The Python prototype copies the host stack on updates; a browser array
   439	   can push/pop in place while preserving the same semantics.
   440	
   441	## Reproduction and provenance
   442	
   443	- `manifests/probe.json` and `manifests/counted.json` bind the original code hashes and job arguments. The latter
   444	  refers to commit `61e6f44`; its four recorded call IDs were resumed by collection, not relaunched at takeover.
   445	- `manifests/l4.json` records the later L4 deployment's hashes. The H100 and L4 executor sources are identical;
   446	  the later source bundle additionally includes the offline report and validation scripts.
   447	- Run `nice -n 19 python3 -m unittest discover -s research/stack -p 'test_stack_*.py' -v`,
   448	  `nice -n 19 python3 research/stack/stack_validate.py --out research/stack/results/validation.json`, then
   449	  `nice -n 19 python3 research/stack/stack_report.py` to reproduce the local checks and Results section.
   450	- Remote protocol is deploy → plan → spawn → collect. Use the recorded manifests and ledger; do not re-spawn
   451	  completed/running IDs. `collect` uses `FunctionCall.get(timeout=0)`, returns immediately for running calls,
   452	  and settles completed calls. Long deployment commands are detached with logs; no foreground server is needed.
   453	- The ledger reserves GPU + maximum CPU/RAM rates plus startup before launch, caps total commitments at $20,
   454	  and settles from recorded wall time. The final report lists all jobs, including failures if any.

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""The canonical-KV pushdown executor on a merged Savant r3, the naive KV stack it is compared with, and the
     2	plain-history decoder. Torch is imported inside the functions that need it. Nothing here launches a job.
     3	
     4	Canonical step: one forward of the block [top][q][a] at positions |Π|, |Π|+1, |Π|+2 over a clone of Π's cache.
     5	This is the pinned tick (scan_forward.single_lookup) with a 3-token block; the frame's KV is slot |Π| of that
     6	forward, recomputed from (Π, top) every step.
     7	"""
     8	
     9	from __future__ import annotations
    10	
    11	import copy
    12	import random
    13	import time
    14	
    15	import stack_machine as M
    16	
    17	SEED = M.SEED
    18	
    19	
    20	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- symbols and Π
    21	
    22	
    23	def assign(pool, name, roles, j):
    24	    """Role -> name. j = 0 takes the test pool in order; j > 0 a seeded sample of it."""
    25	    test = list(pool["test"])
    26	    if len(test) < len(roles):
    27	        raise RuntimeError("test pool too small")
    28	    words = test[:len(roles)] if j == 0 else random.Random(f"{SEED}:{name}:assign:{j}").sample(test, len(roles))
    29	    return {role: words[i] for i, role in enumerate(roles)}
    30	
    31	
    32	def machine_spec(pool, name, j, records=None):
    33	    program = M.programs()[name]
    34	    records = records if records is not None else M.row_records(program)
    35	    roles = M.roles_for(name, records)
    36	    words = assign(pool, name, roles, j)
    37	    ids = {r: int(pool["id_of"][w]) for r, w in words.items()}
    38	    if len(set(ids.values())) != len(ids):
    39	        raise RuntimeError("two roles share a token")
    40	    nl = int(pool["nl"])
    41	    pi = []
    42	    for r in records:
    43	        pi += [ids[r["top"]], ids[r["q"]], ids[r["a"]], ids[r["q2"]], nl]
    44	    return {"name": name, "program": program, "records": records, "words": words, "ids": ids,
    45	            "inv": {v: k for k, v in ids.items()}, "pi": pi, "P": len(pi), "assign": j}
    46	
    47	
    48	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- caches
    49	
    50	
    51	def _layers(cache):
    52	    if hasattr(cache, "layers"):
    53	        return [(layer.keys, layer.values) for layer in cache.layers]
    54	    return list(zip(cache.key_cache, cache.value_cache))
    55	
    56	
    57	def frame_at(cache, slot):
    58	    """(L, 2, H, D) KV of one cache slot, all layers."""
    59	    import torch
    60	    return torch.stack([torch.stack([k[0, :, slot, :], v[0, :, slot, :]]) for k, v in _layers(cache)])
    61	
    62	
    63	def with_frame(past, frame):
    64	    """A new cache: Π's tensors followed by one frame slot. Π is not written."""
    65	    import torch
    66	    new = copy.copy(past)
    67	    if hasattr(past, "layers"):
    68	        new.layers = []
    69	        for i, layer in enumerate(past.layers):
    70	            c = copy.copy(layer)
    71	            c.keys = torch.cat([layer.keys, frame[i, 0][None, :, None, :]], dim=-2)
    72	            c.values = torch.cat([layer.values, frame[i, 1][None, :, None, :]], dim=-2)
    73	            new.layers.append(c)
    74	        return new
    75	    new.key_cache = [torch.cat([k, frame[i, 0][None, :, None, :]], dim=-2) for i, k in enumerate(past.key_cache)]
    76	    new.value_cache = [torch.cat([v, frame[i, 1][None, :, None, :]], dim=-2) for i, v in enumerate(past.value_cache)]
    77	    return new
    78	
    79	
    80	def crop(cache, n):
    81	    if hasattr(cache, "layers"):
    82	        for layer in cache.layers:
    83	            layer.keys = layer.keys[:, :, :n, :]
    84	            layer.values = layer.values[:, :, :n, :]
    85	    else:
    86	        cache.key_cache = [k[:, :, :n, :] for k in cache.key_cache]
    87	        cache.value_cache = [v[:, :, :n, :] for v in cache.value_cache]
    88	
    89	
    90	def seq_len(cache):
    91	    return int(_layers(cache)[0][0].shape[-2])
    92	
    93	
    94	def forward(model, cache, ids, positions, cache_index):
    95	    import torch
    96	    device = next(model.parameters()).device
    97	    pos = torch.tensor([positions], device=device)
    98	    cpos = torch.tensor(list(range(cache_index, cache_index + len(ids))), device=device)
    99	    with torch.inference_mode():
   100	        out = model(input_ids=torch.tensor([ids], device=device), position_ids=pos, cache_position=cpos,
   101	                    past_key_values=cache, use_cache=True)
   102	    return out.logits[0, -1]
   103	
   104	
   105	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the canonical step
   106	
   107	
   108	def step(model, past, P, top_id, q_id, a_id):
   109	    """Logits at the event and the frame's KV (slot P). Same call as scan_forward.single_lookup."""
   110	    import pb_pin
   111	    cache = pb_pin._clone(past)
   112	    logit = forward(model, cache, [top_id, q_id, a_id], [P, P + 1, P + 2], P).clone()
   113	    frame = frame_at(cache, P)
   114	    del cache
   115	    return logit, frame
   116	
   117	
   118	def template(model, past, P, top_id):
   119	    """The frame symbol alone at position P over Π: a 1-token forward."""
   120	    import pb_pin
   121	    cache = pb_pin._clone(past)
   122	    forward(model, cache, [top_id], [P], P)
   123	    frame = frame_at(cache, P)
   124	    del cache
   125	    return frame
   126	
   127	
   128	def top2(logit):
   129	    import torch
   130	    v, i = torch.topk(logit.float(), 2)
   131	    return int(i[0]), float(v[0] - v[1])
   132	
   133	
   134	def certify(model, past, spec):
   135	    """Every reachable row, one canonical step each. Keeps the logits and the frame KV per frame symbol."""
   136	    import torch
   137	    P, ids = spec["P"], spec["ids"]
   138	    logits, frames, rows = {}, {}, []
   139	    frame_equal = {}
   140	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   141	    for r in spec["records"]:
   142	        lg, fr = step(model, past, P, ids[r["top"]], ids[r["q"]], ids[r["a"]])
   143	        arg, margin = top2(lg)
   144	        target = ids[r["q2"]]
   145	        ok = arg == target and margin > 0
   146	        tgt_logit = float(lg[target].float())
   147	        rows.append({"q": r["q"], "top": r["top"], "a": r["a"], "q2": r["q2"], "kind": r["kind"],
   148	                     "argmax": arg, "argmax_role": spec["inv"].get(arg), "margin": margin, "ok": ok,
   149	                     "target_logit": tgt_logit, "max_logit": float(lg.float().max())})
   150	        logits[(r["top"], r["q"], r["a"])] = lg
   151	        if r["top"] not in frames:
   152	            frames[r["top"]] = fr
   153	            frame_equal[r["top"]] = [0, 0]
   154	        frame_equal[r["top"]][0] += 1
   155	        frame_equal[r["top"]][1] += int(torch.equal(fr, frames[r["top"]]))
   156	    seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
   157	    tmpl = {}
   158	    tmpl_equal = {}
   159	    for sym in frames:
   160	        t = template(model, past, P, ids[sym])
   161	        tmpl[sym] = t
   162	        tmpl_equal[sym] = {"bitwise": bool(torch.equal(t, frames[sym])),
   163	                           "max_abs": float((t.float() - frames[sym].float()).abs().max())}
   164	    spec["templates"] = tmpl
   165	    kinds = {}
   166	    for row in rows:
   167	        k = kinds.setdefault(row["kind"], {"n": 0, "ok": 0, "min_margin": None})
   168	        k["n"] += 1
   169	        k["ok"] += int(row["ok"])
   170	        k["min_margin"] = row["margin"] if k["min_margin"] is None else min(k["min_margin"], row["margin"])
   171	    rec = {"machine": spec["name"], "assign": spec["assign"], "words": spec["words"], "ids": spec["ids"],
   172	           "P": P, "n_rows": len(rows), "n_ok": sum(r["ok"] for r in rows),
   173	           "pass": all(r["ok"] for r in rows), "min_margin": min(r["margin"] for r in rows),
   174	           "kinds": kinds, "seconds": seconds, "ms_per_row": 1000 * seconds / len(rows),
   175	           "frame_slot_equal_across_rows": {k: {"n": v[0], "bitwise": v[1]} for k, v in frame_equal.items()},
   176	           "frame_slot_vs_1token_template": tmpl_equal,
   177	           "misses": [r for r in rows if not r["ok"]][:40], "rows": rows}
   178	    return rec, logits, frames
   179	
   180	
   181	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- runs
   182	
   183	
   184	def run_canonical(model, past, spec, tokens, cert_logits, frames, stop_on_error=True):
   185	    """The certified executor. Every step is real. Checks each step's logits and frame KV bitwise."""
   186	    import torch
   187	    program, ids, inv, P = spec["program"], spec["ids"], spec["inv"], spec["P"]
   188	    delta = program["delta"]
   189	    q = program["start_q"]
   190	    stack = [program["start_top"]]
   191	    n_bit_logit = n_bit_frame = n_cert = 0
   192	    max_delta = 0.0
   193	    max_frame_delta = 0.0
   194	    min_margin = None
   195	    first_error = None
   196	    error = None
   197	    depth_max = 0
   198	    seen = {}
   199	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   200	    n = 0
   201	    for i, a in enumerate(tokens):
   202	        top = stack[-1]
   203	        lg, fr = step(model, past, P, ids[top], ids[q], ids[a])
   204	        arg, margin = top2(lg)
   205	        n += 1
   206	        key = (top, q, a)
   207	        ref = cert_logits.get(key)
   208	        if ref is not None:
   209	            n_cert += 1
   210	            if torch.equal(lg, ref):
   211	                n_bit_logit += 1
   212	            else:
   213	                max_delta = max(max_delta, float((lg.float() - ref.float()).abs().max()))
   214	        if torch.equal(fr, frames[top]):
   215	            n_bit_frame += 1
   216	        else:
   217	            max_frame_delta = max(max_frame_delta, float((fr.float() - frames[top].float()).abs().max()))
   218	        min_margin = margin if min_margin is None else min(min_margin, margin)
   219	        s = seen.setdefault(top, [10 ** 9, -1, 0])
   220	        d = len(stack) - 1
   221	        s[0], s[1], s[2] = min(s[0], d), max(s[1], d), s[2] + 1
   222	        want_q2, op = delta(q, top, a)
   223	        got = inv.get(arg)
   224	        if got != want_q2:
   225	            if first_error is None:
   226	                first_error = i
   227	                error = {"step": i, "q": q, "top": top, "a": a, "want": want_q2, "got": got, "argmax": arg,
   228	                         "margin": margin, "depth": d}
   229	            if stop_on_error:
   230	                break
   231	        if op[0] == "pop" and len(stack) < 2:
   232	            first_error = i if first_error is None else first_error
   233	            error = error or {"step": i, "why": "pop on BOT"}
   234	            break
   235	        stack = list(M.apply(tuple(stack), op)) if op[0] != "stay" else stack
   236	        q = want_q2 if got == want_q2 else (got or q)
   237	        depth_max = max(depth_max, len(stack) - 1)
   238	    seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
   239	    final = q
   240	    return {"n_steps": n, "n_tokens": len(tokens), "exact": first_error is None and n == len(tokens),
   241	            "final_q": final, "value": M.value_of(program, final), "first_error": first_error, "error": error,
   242	            "n_cert_rows": n_cert, "n_logits_bitwise": n_bit_logit, "n_frames_bitwise": n_bit_frame,
   243	            "max_abs_delta": max_delta, "max_frame_delta": max_frame_delta, "min_margin": min_margin, "max_depth": depth_max,
   244	            "frame_depths": {k: {"min_depth": v[0], "max_depth": v[1], "n": v[2]} for k, v in seen.items()},
   245	            "seconds": seconds, "ms_per_step": 1000 * seconds / max(1, n)}
   246	
   247	
   248	def run_history(model, spec, tokens, max_context=40960):
   249	    """Plain decoding with history: Π, then the transcript ` top q a q2\\n`, positions not restarted. The host
   250	    supplies top from its stack; q is the model's own previous output. Stops at the first wrong state."""
   251	    import scan_forward as fwd
   252	    program, ids, inv = spec["program"], spec["ids"], spec["inv"]
   253	    delta = program["delta"]
   254	    nl = spec["pi"][4]
   255	    need = spec["P"] + 5 * len(tokens)
   256	    if need > max_context:
   257	        return {"runnable": False, "context_needed": need, "n_tokens": len(tokens)}
   258	    cache = fwd.prefill(model, spec["pi"])
   259	    L = spec["P"]
   260	    q = program["start_q"]
   261	    stack = [program["start_top"]]
   262	    pending = []
   263	    first_error = None
   264	    error = None
   265	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   266	    n = 0
   267	    for i, a in enumerate(tokens):
   268	        top = stack[-1]
   269	        feed = pending + [ids[top], ids[q], ids[a]]
   270	        lg = forward(model, cache, feed, list(range(L, L + len(feed))), L)
   271	        L += len(feed)
   272	        arg, margin = top2(lg)
   273	        n += 1
   274	        want_q2, op = delta(q, top, a)
   275	        got = inv.get(arg)
   276	        if got != want_q2:
   277	            first_error = i
   278	            error = {"step": i, "q": q, "top": top, "a": a, "want": want_q2, "got": got, "argmax": arg,
   279	                     "margin": margin, "depth": len(stack) - 1}
   280	            break
   281	        stack = list(M.apply(tuple(stack), op)) if op[0] != "stay" else stack
   282	        q = want_q2
   283	        pending = [arg, nl]
   284	    seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
   285	    del cache
   286	    return {"runnable": True, "n_steps": n, "n_tokens": len(tokens), "exact": first_error is None,
   287	            "first_error": first_error, "error": error, "final_q": q if first_error is None else None,
   288	            "seconds": seconds, "ms_per_step": 1000 * seconds / max(1, n), "context_needed": need}
   289	
   290	
   291	def naive_check(model, past, spec, tokens, frames, variant, every=1, max_steps=None):
   292	    """Keep each frame's original KV: the stack is a cache Π ⊕ frames, a push runs the frame over Π and every frame
   293	    below it (variant B at position P, variant A at P + depth), a pop crops. Templates are the 1-token forwards
   294	    `certify` stores in spec["templates"]; `frames` is unused here and kept for the call signature. Each sampled step compares the stored
   295	    top frame with the canonical template, and a 2-token step [q][a] over Π ⊕ stored frame with the same step over
   296	    Π ⊕ template (the control). The trajectory is the reference one, so errors do not compound."""
   297	    import pb_pin
   298	    program, ids, P = spec["program"], spec["ids"], spec["P"]
   299	    delta = program["delta"]
   300	    cache = pb_pin._clone(past)
   301	
   302	    def push(sym):
   303	        depth = seq_len(cache) - P
   304	        pos = P if variant == "B" else P + depth
   305	        forward(model, cache, [ids[sym]], [pos], seq_len(cache))
   306	
   307	    q = program["start_q"]
   308	    stack = [program["start_top"]]
   309	    push(stack[0])
   310	    out = {"variant": variant, "by_depth": {}, "n": 0}
   311	    for i, a in enumerate(tokens):
   312	        if max_steps is not None and i >= max_steps:
   313	            break
   314	        top = stack[-1]
   315	        want_q2, op = delta(q, top, a)
   316	        d = len(stack) - 1
   317	        if i % every == 0:
   318	            stored = frame_at(cache, seq_len(cache) - 1)
   319	            # the 1-token template has the same forward shape as a naive push, so depth 0 is bitwise
   320	            tmpl = spec["templates"][top]
   321	            dk = float((stored[:, 0].float() - tmpl[:, 0].float()).abs().max())
   322	            dv = float((stored[:, 1].float() - tmpl[:, 1].float()).abs().max())
   323	            rel = float((stored.float() - tmpl.float()).norm() / tmpl.float().norm().clamp(min=1e-8))
   324	            qpos = P + 1 if variant == "B" else P + d + 1
   325	            lg_n = forward(model, with_frame(past, stored), [ids[q], ids[a]], [qpos, qpos + 1], P + 1).clone()
   326	            lg_c = forward(model, with_frame(past, tmpl), [ids[q], ids[a]], [P + 1, P + 2], P + 1).clone()
   327	            arg_n, m_n = top2(lg_n)
   328	            arg_c, m_c = top2(lg_c)
   329	            bucket = "0" if d == 0 else "1-7" if d < 8 else "8-63" if d < 64 else "64-511" if d < 512 else "512+"
   330	            b = out["by_depth"].setdefault(bucket, {"n": 0, "kv_bitwise": 0, "max_dk": 0.0, "max_dv": 0.0,
   331	                                                    "max_rel": 0.0, "naive_ok": 0, "control_ok": 0,
   332	                                                    "max_dlogit": 0.0, "argmax_differs": 0})
   333	            b["n"] += 1
   334	            b["kv_bitwise"] += int(dk == 0.0 and dv == 0.0)
   335	            b["max_dk"] = max(b["max_dk"], dk)
   336	            b["max_dv"] = max(b["max_dv"], dv)
   337	            b["max_rel"] = max(b["max_rel"], rel)
   338	            b["naive_ok"] += int(arg_n == ids[want_q2])
   339	            b["control_ok"] += int(arg_c == ids[want_q2])
   340	            b["max_dlogit"] = max(b["max_dlogit"], float((lg_n.float() - lg_c.float()).abs().max()))
   341	            b["argmax_differs"] += int(arg_n != arg_c)
   342	            out["n"] += 1
   343	        # host stack and the KV stack move together
   344	        if op[0] == "push":
   345	            stack.append(op[1])
   346	            push(op[1])
   347	        elif op[0] == "pop":
   348	            stack.pop()
   349	            crop(cache, seq_len(cache) - 1)
   350	        elif op[0] == "rewrite":
   351	            stack[-1] = op[1]
   352	            crop(cache, seq_len(cache) - 1)
   353	            push(op[1])
   354	        elif op[0] == "store_push":
   355	            stack[-1] = op[1]
   356	            crop(cache, seq_len(cache) - 1)
   357	            push(op[1])
   358	            stack.append(op[2])
   359	            push(op[2])
   360	        q = want_q2
   361	    del cache
   362	    return out

   284	**Depth-0 numerical control:** Dyck-4's naive path is not always bitwise equal even at BOT: 25/41 matches for B and 18/41 for A, with max abs ΔK 0.125 and ΔV 0.15625. All these depth-0 argmaxes remain correct, and the maximum logit difference is 0.25. The cause of this small drift was not isolated, so not every nonzero naive difference can be attributed solely to history. Nonbottom differences are much larger and include wrong states, while the separate three-token canonical path is bitwise on every counted step.

   349	### Speed
   350	
   351	- **H100:** 24.02 ms per canonical step, weighted by steps over the 15 counted cells (90459 steps). This includes the bitwise checks of the logits and the frame.
   352	- **L4:** 63.97 ms per step over the d8, d64 and d512 cells of all three machines (5870 steps). Every step was exact: True. The L4 certified its own rows: Dyck-2 50/50, Dyck-4 144/144, Boolean 294/294. Bits are per host and code path, so an L4 deployment needs its own certificate, and it has one.
   353	
   354	### Registered predictions: outcomes
   355	
   356	- **Canonical history independence: holds on the counted path.** Every counted logit and frame check is bitwise, including depth 4,096. The shape-dependent 1-token template is not substituted into this path.
   357	- **Certificates:** first-assignment Dyck-2 success holds; predicted zero-shot Boolean failure misses. All three certify at assignment 0. The conditional assignment search and ≤300-step LoRA were unnecessary.
   358	- **Runs:** all 15 cells, 72 random instances, all malformed cases and official BBH 250 pass the offline audit, including trace correctness, intended depth and input hashes. Depth 4,096 has one registered cell per machine; the eight-instance random suites cover depths 8, 64 and 512.
   359	- **Dyck-2 naive:** nonbottom sampled KV bitwise matches 0/6104; A errors at depth ≥512: 4/8. Total B errors 19/3090, A errors 227/3090; B≤A prediction holds. The per-depth table reports sampled bucket maxima, not a bound for all possible naive caches.
   360	- **Dyck-4 naive:** nonbottom sampled KV bitwise matches 0/6098; A errors at depth ≥512: 2/19. Total B errors 294/3090, A errors 67/3090; B≤A prediction misses. The per-depth table reports sampled bucket maxima, not a bound for all possible naive caches.
   361	- **Boolean naive:** nonbottom sampled KV bitwise matches 0/4472; A errors at depth ≥512: 0/2. Total B errors 132/2270, A errors 77/2270; B≤A prediction misses. The per-depth table reports sampled bucket maxima, not a bound for all possible naive caches.
   362	- **Naive A fails at depth 512 on at least one machine:** holds (6 sampled errors across machines).
   363	- **History fails on at least one machine's d64 random suite:** misses (0/24 failed traces).
   364	- **Dyck-2 comparators:** history fails 0/8 at d64; d512 <50% exact prediction misses. Thinking d8 ≥75% prediction misses (5/8); d512 <75% prediction holds (0/8).
   365	- **Dyck-4 comparators:** history fails 0/8 at d64; d512 <50% exact prediction misses. Thinking d8 ≥75% prediction misses (4/8); d512 <75% prediction holds (4/8).
   366	- **Boolean comparators:** history fails 0/8 at d64; d512 <50% exact prediction misses. Thinking d8 ≥75% prediction holds (8/8); d512 <75% prediction misses (6/8).
   367	- **Speed:** H100 24.02 ms <50 ms prediction holds; L4 63.97 ms <150 ms prediction holds. These include auditing overhead and are not optimized kernel benchmarks.
   368	
   369	### Scope and limitations
   370	
   371	The host supplies the program, compiles BBH syntax, maintains the symbolic stack and applies its operations. The model selects the next control state by full-vocabulary argmax at each real step. This is execution with a supplied compiler/table, not autonomous program synthesis. The history comparator also receives the host stack; the thinking comparator answers in natural language. The thinking runs cover d8/d64/d512 only, not d4096 or L20000. History cells exceeding its context budget are explicitly not runnable. No memory/latency advantage over StackTrans, stack attention, or fresh calls is measured, and no browser runtime is benchmarked.
   372	
   373	Zero-shot here means no additional stack-specific training on top of Savant r3; the pre-existing Savant adapter is part of the tested model. Short history-decoder successes are reported as successes; the result does not imply that canonical execution beats history on every input.
   374	
   375	The proof is conditional on deterministic execution of the fixed inference path and on the trusted host machine. Sampled bitwise checks support that premise; they do not prove arbitrary hardware determinism. A maximum logit deviation observed on test runs is an empirical tolerance, not a uniform bound on untested numerical perturbations. Model, dtype, quantization, runtime, GPU, batching, position policy or cache-layout changes require a new certificate.
   376

   392	
   393	## Browser port: exact implementation and acceptance steps
   394	
   395	This is a port specification; no browser implementation or browser speed claim is included in this experiment.
   396	
   397	1. **Freeze the artifact.** Merge `arith-s1r2/adapter_step500` into `Qwen/Qwen3-4B` using
   398	   `scan_forward.load_merged` / `pb_eval.merge_adapter`. Export those merged weights, configuration and tokenizer
   399	   to a browser GPU backend that exposes explicit per-layer past K/V, position IDs and attention masks. Record
   400	   file hashes, precision/quantization, backend version and GPU identity. A backend that only offers a chat or
   401	   generation API is insufficient. The current bf16 model needs a substantial device-memory budget; any smaller
   402	   quantized export is a new model configuration and must pass its own certificate.
   403	2. **Export the machine, not natural-language prompts.** Serialize `stack_machine.row_records` in its sorted
   404	   order and `stack_forward.machine_spec` with assignment 0: role→token IDs, newline ID, Π, start state, BOT,
   405	   halting states and each row's stack operation. Use the saved `cert.json` IDs to check the export. Each Π row
   406	   is exactly `[top, q, a, q2, newline]`; P is 250/720/1,470 for Dyck-2/Dyck-4/Boolean. No chat template, BOS,
   407	   padding or tokenizer-added special tokens may enter these integer sequences.
   408	3. **Port the trusted host machine.** Use an array of integer frame symbols initialized to BOT and one control
   409	   state. Translate `apply` operations `stay`, `rewrite`, `push`, `pop`, `store_push`; prohibit popping BOT.
   410	   Preserve child-result transfer in `bool_delta`. Port the closed-cover check and reference tests before GPU
   411	   inference. For infix Boolean text, use an iterative parser with `not > and > or` precedence, accepting only
   412	   Boolean literals/operators/parentheses, then emit the same prefix tokens and final END. Reject unknown tokens
   413	   explicitly. Validate the parser on all 250 pinned BBH inputs and the random-expression tests.
   414	4. **Prefill once.** Run Π at positions 0…P−1 with a causal mask; retain its per-layer K/V as immutable buffers.
   415	   Disable dropout and sampling. Allocate separate scratch for exactly three query tokens. The physical cache
   416	   indices and rotary positions of those tokens are P, P+1, P+2. Queries may attend to all Π and their own causal
   417	   prefix of the three-token block, never the external stack or an earlier step's scratch. Reuse buffers only
   418	   when the runtime guarantees the immutable Π prefix is not overwritten.
   419	5. **Implement the same three-token step.** Feed `[top_id, state_id, event_id]` as one block over Π. Use the
   420	   full-vocabulary argmax at the event position as the next state. Apply the host operation for the *incoming*
   421	   `(q, top, event)` row; do not replace the model's output with the reference target. Discard all three scratch
   422	   slots. Do not split the top token into a one-token prefill: that is a different numerical path. The step's
   423	   frame KV is scratch slot P across every layer, and the stack stores symbols, never those vectors.
   424	6. **Certify on the actual browser device.** Evaluate all 50/144/294 rows with the same batch size, shapes,
   425	   precision, mask and cache path as deployment. Require every full-vocabulary winner to equal its target with
   426	   strictly positive winner–runner-up margin. Retain logits per row and a slot-P frame per symbol; compare frames
   427	   across all rows sharing that symbol. A desktop H100/L4 certificate cannot authorize a browser export. Stop
   428	   on any failure; do not silently use a host lookup of the target state.
   429	7. **Run the port acceptance suite.** Replay the registered cells (depths 8/64/512/4,096 and L20000), all 72 random
   430	   instances, malformed inputs and BBH 250. Check each state against the reference and each full logit vector
   431	   and frame against the browser certificate, as `run_canonical` does. Hash Π before/after runs to catch cache
   432	   mutation. Repeat after changing depth/history and input order. If bitwise equality fails, report the observed
   433	   deviations and recertify the actual path; do not turn a sampled maximum into a universal bound.
   434	8. **Measure and retain provenance.** Run inference in a Web Worker, keep Π on the GPU, and yield progress between
   435	   short chunks of steps. Measure synchronized wall milliseconds per step and peak device memory separately
   436	   with and without auditing overhead. Log artifact hashes, certificate margins, device/runtime versions and
   437	   input hashes. Re-certify after any configuration change. Attention cache size is fixed by P+3; host stack
   438	   storage grows with nesting depth. The Python prototype copies the host stack on updates; a browser array
   439	   can push/pop in place while preserving the same semantics.

     1	"""One STACK GPU job: probe (certify, choose assignments, smoke every path), counted (one machine, everything),
     2	l4 (certify and time on an L4), think (base Qwen3-4B with thinking, vLLM). Writes JSON under --out.

    10	import re

    19	BBH_PATH = "/opt/bbh/bbh/boolean_expressions.json"
    20	N_ASSIGN = 16

    23	def load(adapter2=None):
    24	    import scan_forward as fwd
    25	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    26	    tok = fwd.load_tokenizer()
    27	    model = fwd.load_merged()
    28	    if adapter2:
    29	        from peft import PeftModel
    30	        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, adapter2).merge_and_unload().eval()
    31	    import pb_pin
    32	    pool = pb_pin.name_pool(tok)
    33	    print(f"loaded in {time.perf_counter() - t0:.1f}s adapter2={adapter2}", flush=True)
    34	    return model, tok, pool
    35	
    36	
    37	def public_cert(rec):
    38	    return {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k != "rows"} | {"rows": rec["rows"]}
    39	
    40	
    41	def cert_summary(rec):
    42	    return {k: rec[k] for k in ("machine", "assign", "words", "P", "n_rows", "n_ok", "pass", "min_margin", "kinds",
    43	                                "ms_per_row", "frame_slot_equal_across_rows", "frame_slot_vs_1token_template")} | {
    44	        "misses": [{k: m[k] for k in ("q", "top", "a", "q2", "kind", "argmax_role", "argmax", "margin")}
    45	                   for m in rec["misses"][:12]]}
    46	
    47	
    48	def parse_assign(text):
    49	    out = {}
    50	    for part in filter(None, text.split(",")):
    51	        k, v = part.split("=")
    52	        out[k] = int(v)
    53	    return out
    54	
    55	
    56	def setup(model, pool, name, j):
    57	    import scan_forward as fwd
    58	    spec = sf.machine_spec(pool, name, j)
    59	    past = fwd.prefill(model, spec["pi"])
    60	    rec, logits, frames = sf.certify(model, past, spec)
    61	    return spec, past, rec, logits, frames
    62

    67	def probe(out, assign_text):
    68	    import scan_forward as fwd
    69	    model, tok, pool = load()
    70	    host = host_record()
    71	    fixed = parse_assign(assign_text)
    72	    res = {"seed": M.SEED, "host": host, "machines": {}}
    73	    for name in ("dyck2", "dyck4", "bool"):
    74	        tries = []
    75	        chosen = None
    76	        for j in ([fixed[name]] if name in fixed else range(N_ASSIGN)):
    77	            spec, past, rec, logits, frames = setup(model, pool, name, j)
    78	            tries.append(cert_summary(rec))
    79	            print(f"probe {name} assign={j} {rec['n_ok']}/{rec['n_rows']} min_margin={rec['min_margin']:.3f} "
    80	                  f"ms_row={rec['ms_per_row']:.1f}", flush=True)
    81	            for m in rec["misses"][:6]:
    82	                print(f"   miss {m['q']} {m['top']} {m['a']} -> want {m['q2']} got {m['argmax_role']} "
    83	                      f"({m['argmax']}) margin {m['margin']:.3f} kind {m['kind']}", flush=True)
    84	            if rec["pass"]:
    85	                chosen = j
    86	                break
    87	            del past, logits, frames
    88	            fwd.torch_empty()
    89	        entry = {"tries": tries, "chosen": chosen}
    90	        if chosen is not None:
    91	            cell = M.cell_input(name, M.CELLS[0])
    92	            entry["d8_canonical"] = sf.run_canonical(model, past, spec, cell["tokens"], logits, frames)
    93	            entry["d8_history"] = sf.run_history(model, spec, cell["tokens"])
    94	            inst = M.instances(name, 8, 2)
    95	            entry["naive_B_d8"] = sf.naive_check(model, past, spec, inst[0]["tokens"], frames, "B")
    96	            entry["naive_A_d8"] = sf.naive_check(model, past, spec, inst[0]["tokens"], frames, "A")
    97	            print(f"probe {name} d8 exact={entry['d8_canonical']['exact']} "
    98	                  f"bitwise={entry['d8_canonical']['n_logits_bitwise']}/{entry['d8_canonical']['n_cert_rows']} "
    99	                  f"frames={entry['d8_canonical']['n_frames_bitwise']} ms={entry['d8_canonical']['ms_per_step']:.1f} "
   100	                  f"history_exact={entry['d8_history'].get('exact')} "
   101	                  f"naiveB={entry['naive_B_d8']['by_depth']}", flush=True)
   102	            del past, logits, frames
   103	            fwd.torch_empty()
   104	        res["machines"][name] = entry
   105	        atomic_json(out / "probe.json", res)
   106

   111	def counted(out, name, j, adapter2=None):
   112	    import scan_forward as fwd
   113	    model, tok, pool = load(adapter2)
   114	    host = host_record()
   115	    spec, past, rec, logits, frames = setup(model, pool, name, j)
   116	    base = {"seed": M.SEED, "host": host, "machine": name, "assign": j, "adapter2": adapter2}
   117	    atomic_json(out / "cert.json", base | {"cert": public_cert(rec)})
   118	    print(f"cert {name} assign={j} {rec['n_ok']}/{rec['n_rows']} min_margin={rec['min_margin']:.3f}", flush=True)
   119	    if not rec["pass"]:
   120	        sys.exit(3)
   121	    program = spec["program"]
   122	
   123	    def canon(tokens):
   124	        return sf.run_canonical(model, past, spec, tokens, logits, frames)
   125	
   126	    cells = []
   127	    for c in M.CELLS:
   128	        made = M.cell_input(name, c)
   129	        ref = M.run(program, made["tokens"])
   130	        got = canon(made["tokens"])
   131	        got.update({"cell": c["cell"], "ref_value": M.value_of(program, ref["q"]), "ref_max_depth": ref["max_depth"],
   132	                    "token_sha256": M.token_hash(made["tokens"])})
   133	        got["verdict_ok"] = got["exact"] and got["value"] is got["ref_value"]
   134	        cells.append(got)
   135	        atomic_json(out / "cells.json", base | {"cells": cells})
   136	        print(f"cell {c['cell']} steps={got['n_steps']} exact={got['exact']} depth={got['max_depth']} "
   137	              f"bitwise={got['n_logits_bitwise']}/{got['n_cert_rows']} frames={got['n_frames_bitwise']} "
   138	              f"ms={got['ms_per_step']:.2f}", flush=True)
   139	    malformed = []
   140	    for label, tokens in M.malformed_tokens(name).items():
   141	        got = canon(tokens)
   142	        ref = M.run(program, tokens)
   143	        got.update({"label": label, "ref_final": ref["q"]})
   144	        got["ends_in_ref_sink"] = got["exact"] and got["final_q"] == ref["q"]
   145	        malformed.append(got)
   146	    atomic_json(out / "malformed.json", base | {"malformed": malformed})
   147	    inst_rows = []
   148	    for depth in M.INSTANCE_DEPTHS:
   149	        for x in M.instances(name, depth):
   150	            got = canon(x["tokens"])
   151	            got.update({"depth": depth, "i": x["i"], "ref_value": x["value"], "token_sha256": x["token_sha256"]})
   152	            got["verdict_ok"] = got["exact"] and got["value"] is x["value"]
   153	            inst_rows.append(got)
   154	        atomic_json(out / "instances.json", base | {"instances": inst_rows})
   155	        print(f"instances d{depth} exact={sum(r['exact'] for r in inst_rows if r['depth'] == depth)}/"
   156	              f"{sum(r['depth'] == depth for r in inst_rows)}", flush=True)
   157	    if name == "bool":
   158	        bbh(out, base, model, past, spec, logits, frames)
   159	    naive = {"A": [], "B": []}
   160	    for depth in M.INSTANCE_DEPTHS:
   161	        xs = M.instances(name, depth)
   162	        use = xs if depth < 512 else xs[:2]
   163	        every = 1 if depth < 512 else 4
   164	        for variant in ("B", "A"):
   165	            for x in use:
   166	                got = sf.naive_check(model, past, spec, x["tokens"], frames, variant, every=every)
   167	                got.update({"depth": depth, "i": x["i"]})
   168	                naive[variant].append(got)
   169	        atomic_json(out / "naive.json", base | {"naive": naive})
   170	        print(f"naive d{depth} done", flush=True)
   171	    hist = {"instances": [], "cells": []}
   172	    for depth in M.INSTANCE_DEPTHS:
   173	        for x in M.instances(name, depth):
   174	            got = sf.run_history(model, spec, x["tokens"])
   175	            got.update({"depth": depth, "i": x["i"]})
   176	            hist["instances"].append(got)
   177	        atomic_json(out / "history.json", base | {"history": hist})
   178	        print(f"history d{depth} exact={sum(bool(r.get('exact')) for r in hist['instances'] if r['depth'] == depth)}"
   179	              f"/{sum(r['depth'] == depth for r in hist['instances'])}", flush=True)
   180	    for c in M.CELLS:
   181	        made = M.cell_input(name, c)
   182	        got = sf.run_history(model, spec, made["tokens"])
   183	        got.update({"cell": c["cell"]})
   184	        hist["cells"].append(got)
   185	        atomic_json(out / "history.json", base | {"history": hist})
   186	    atomic_json(out / "run.json", base | {"completed": True})
   187	    del past
   188	    fwd.torch_empty()

   191	def bbh(out, base, model, past, spec, logits, frames):
   192	    examples = json.loads(pathlib.Path(BBH_PATH).read_text())["examples"]
   193	    rows = []
   194	    for i, ex in enumerate(examples):
   195	        tokens, py_val = M.compile_bbh(ex["input"])
   196	        tokens = tokens + ["END"]
   197	        got = sf.run_canonical(model, past, spec, tokens, logits, frames)
   198	        hist = sf.run_history(model, spec, tokens)
   199	        target = ex["target"] == "True"
   200	        rows.append({"i": i, "n_tokens": len(tokens), "target": target, "py": py_val,
   201	                     "canonical_value": got["value"], "canonical_exact": got["exact"],
   202	                     "canonical_correct": got["exact"] and got["value"] is target,
   203	                     "logits_bitwise": got["n_logits_bitwise"], "cert_rows": got["n_cert_rows"],
   204	                     "frames_bitwise": got["n_frames_bitwise"], "ms_per_step": got["ms_per_step"],
   205	                     "max_depth": got["max_depth"], "history_exact": hist.get("exact"),
   206	                     "history_first_error": hist.get("first_error")})
   207	    n_ok = sum(r["canonical_correct"] for r in rows)
   208	    atomic_json(out / "bbh.json", base | {"n": len(rows), "canonical_correct": n_ok,
   209	                                          "history_exact": sum(bool(r["history_exact"]) for r in rows),
   210	                                          "rows": rows})
   211	    print(f"bbh canonical {n_ok}/{len(rows)} history {sum(bool(r['history_exact']) for r in rows)}", flush=True)

   217	def l4(out, assign_text):
   218	    import scan_forward as fwd
   219	    model, tok, pool = load()
   220	    host = host_record()
   221	    fixed = parse_assign(assign_text)
   222	    res = {"seed": M.SEED, "host": host, "machines": {}}
   223	    for name in ("dyck2", "dyck4", "bool"):
   224	        spec, past, rec, logits, frames = setup(model, pool, name, fixed[name])
   225	        entry = {"cert": cert_summary(rec), "cells": []}
   226	        print(f"l4 cert {name} {rec['n_ok']}/{rec['n_rows']} min_margin={rec['min_margin']:.3f}", flush=True)
   227	        if rec["pass"]:
   228	            for c in M.CELLS[:3]:
   229	                made = M.cell_input(name, c)
   230	                got = sf.run_canonical(model, past, spec, made["tokens"], logits, frames)
   231	                got["cell"] = c["cell"]
   232	                got["ref_value"] = M.value_of(spec["program"], M.run(spec["program"], made["tokens"])["q"])
   233	                entry["cells"].append(got)
   234	                print(f"l4 {name} {c['cell']} exact={got['exact']} ms={got['ms_per_step']:.2f}", flush=True)
   235	        res["machines"][name] = entry
   236	        atomic_json(out / "l4.json", res)
   237	        del past, logits, frames
   238	        fwd.torch_empty()

   241	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- base model with thinking
   242	
   243	THINK_TEMPLATE = "<|im_start|>user\n{q}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n"
   244	BRACKETS = {"O0": "(", "C0": ")", "O1": "[", "C1": "]", "O2": "{", "C2": "}", "O3": "<", "C3": ">"}
   245	
   246	
   247	def question(name, tokens):
   248	    body = tokens[:-1]
   249	    if name == "bool":
   250	        expr = M.tokens_to_expr(body)
   251	        return ("Evaluate the following Boolean expression (Python semantics). End your reply with a final line of the "
   252	                f"form 'Answer: True' or 'Answer: False'.\n\n{expr}")
   253	    seq = " ".join(BRACKETS[t] for t in body)
   254	    return ("Is the following sequence of brackets balanced, i.e. is every opening bracket closed by a bracket of the "
   255	            "same type in the correct nesting order, with nothing left open? End your reply with a final line of the "
   256	            f"form 'Answer: Yes' or 'Answer: No'.\n\n{seq}")
   257	
   258	
   259	def parse_answer(name, text):
   260	    if "</think>" not in text:
   261	        return None
   262	    tail = text.split("</think>")[-1]
   263	    hits = re.findall(r"Answer:\s*\**\s*(True|False|Yes|No)", tail)
   264	    if not hits:
   265	        return None
   266	    return hits[-1] in ("True", "Yes")
   267	
   268	
   269	def think(out, max_model_len=32768):
   270	    from tokenizers import Tokenizer
   271	    from huggingface_hub import snapshot_download
   272	    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
   273	    import os
   274	    path = snapshot_download("Qwen/Qwen3-4B")
   275	    tok = Tokenizer.from_file(os.path.join(path, "tokenizer.json"))
   276	    items = []
   277	    for name in ("dyck2", "dyck4", "bool"):
   278	        for depth in M.INSTANCE_DEPTHS:
   279	            for x in M.instances(name, depth):
   280	                items.append({"machine": name, "set": "instances", "depth": depth, "i": x["i"], "tokens": x["tokens"],
   281	                              "value": x["value"]})
   282	        for c in M.CELLS[:3]:
   283	            made = M.cell_input(name, c)
   284	            program = M.programs()[name]
   285	            items.append({"machine": name, "set": "cells", "cell": c["cell"], "depth": c["depth"],
   286	                          "tokens": made["tokens"], "value": M.value_of(program, M.run(program, made["tokens"])["q"])})
   287	    im_end = tok.token_to_id("<|im_end|>")
   288	    prompts, params = [], []
   289	    for i, it in enumerate(items):
   290	        q = question(it["machine"], it["tokens"])
   291	        ids = tok.encode(THINK_TEMPLATE.format(q=q), add_special_tokens=False).ids
   292	        it["prompt_tokens"] = len(ids)
   293	        prompts.append({"prompt_token_ids": ids})
   294	        params.append(SamplingParams(temperature=0.6, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, seed=i, stop_token_ids=[im_end],
   295	                                     max_tokens=max(16, max_model_len - len(ids) - 8), detokenize=False))
   296	    llm = LLM(model="Qwen/Qwen3-4B", dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=max_model_len, gpu_memory_utilization=0.9,
   297	              seed=0)
   298	    t0 = time.time()
   299	    outs = llm.generate(prompts, params)
   300	    wall = time.time() - t0
   301	    rows = []
   302	    for it, o in zip(items, outs):
   303	        ids = list(o.outputs[0].token_ids)
   304	        text = tok.decode(ids, skip_special_tokens=False)
   305	        ans = parse_answer(it["machine"], text)
   306	        rows.append({k: it[k] for k in it if k != "tokens"} | {
   307	            "n_symbols": len(it["tokens"]) - 1, "answer": ans, "correct": ans is it["value"],
   308	            "response_tokens": len(ids), "hit_max": o.outputs[0].finish_reason == "length",
   309	            "tail": text[-400:]})
   310	    atomic_json(out / "think.json", {"seed": M.SEED, "host": host_record(), "wall": wall, "max_model_len": max_model_len,
   311	                                     "sampling": {"temperature": 0.6, "top_p": 0.95, "top_k": 20}, "rows": rows})
   312	    for name in ("dyck2", "dyck4", "bool"):
   313	        for depth in M.INSTANCE_DEPTHS:
   314	            sel = [r for r in rows if r["machine"] == name and r["set"] == "instances" and r["depth"] == depth]
   315	            print(f"think {name} d{depth} {sum(r['correct'] for r in sel)}/{len(sel)}", flush=True)
   316	
   317	
   318	def main():
   319	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   320	    ap.add_argument("--mode", required=True, choices=("probe", "counted", "l4", "think"))
   321	    ap.add_argument("--machine", default="")
   322	    ap.add_argument("--assign", default="")
   323	    ap.add_argument("--adapter2", default="")
   324	    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
   325	    a = ap.parse_args()
   326	    out = pathlib.Path(a.out)
   327	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   328	    try:
   329	        if a.mode == "probe":
   330	            probe(out, a.assign)
   331	        elif a.mode == "counted":
   332	            counted(out, a.machine, parse_assign(a.assign)[a.machine], a.adapter2 or None)
   333	        elif a.mode == "l4":
   334	            l4(out, a.assign)
   335	        else:
   336	            think(out)
   337	    except SystemExit:
   338	        raise
   339	    except Exception:
   340	        atomic_json(out / "error.json", {"error": traceback.format_exc()[-4000:]})
   341	        raise
   342	
   343	
   344	if __name__ == "__main__":
   345	    main()

    33	    print(f"loaded in {time.perf_counter() - t0:.1f}s adapter2={adapter2}", flush=True)
    34	    return model, tok, pool
    35	
    36	
    37	def public_cert(rec):
    38	    return {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k != "rows"} | {"rows": rec["rows"]}
    39	
    40	
    41	def cert_summary(rec):
    42	    return {k: rec[k] for k in ("machine", "assign", "words", "P", "n_rows", "n_ok", "pass", "min_margin", "kinds",
    43	                                "ms_per_row", "frame_slot_equal_across_rows", "frame_slot_vs_1token_template")} | {
    44	        "misses": [{k: m[k] for k in ("q", "top", "a", "q2", "kind", "argmax_role", "argmax", "margin")}
    45	                   for m in rec["misses"][:12]]}
    46	
    47	
    48	def parse_assign(text):
    49	    out = {}
    50	    for part in filter(None, text.split(",")):
    51	        k, v = part.split("=")
    52	        out[k] = int(v)
    53	    return out
    54	
    55	
    56	def setup(model, pool, name, j):
    57	    import scan_forward as fwd
    58	    spec = sf.machine_spec(pool, name, j)
    59	    past = fwd.prefill(model, spec["pi"])
    60	    rec, logits, frames = sf.certify(model, past, spec)
    61	    return spec, past, rec, logits, frames
    62

    64	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- probe

    66	
    67	def probe(out, assign_text):
    68	    import scan_forward as fwd
    69	    model, tok, pool = load()
    70	    host = host_record()
    71	    fixed = parse_assign(assign_text)
    72	    res = {"seed": M.SEED, "host": host, "machines": {}}
    73	    for name in ("dyck2", "dyck4", "bool"):
    74	        tries = []
    75	        chosen = None
    76	        for j in ([fixed[name]] if name in fixed else range(N_ASSIGN)):
    77	            spec, past, rec, logits, frames = setup(model, pool, name, j)
    78	            tries.append(cert_summary(rec))
    79	            print(f"probe {name} assign={j} {rec['n_ok']}/{rec['n_rows']} min_margin={rec['min_margin']:.3f} "
    80	                  f"ms_row={rec['ms_per_row']:.1f}", flush=True)
    81	            for m in rec["misses"][:6]:
    82	                print(f"   miss {m['q']} {m['top']} {m['a']} -> want {m['q2']} got {m['argmax_role']} "
    83	                      f"({m['argmax']}) margin {m['margin']:.3f} kind {m['kind']}", flush=True)
    84	            if rec["pass"]:
    85	                chosen = j
    86	                break
    87	            del past, logits, frames
    88	            fwd.torch_empty()
    89	        entry = {"tries": tries, "chosen": chosen}
    90	        if chosen is not None:
    91	            cell = M.cell_input(name, M.CELLS[0])
    92	            entry["d8_canonical"] = sf.run_canonical(model, past, spec, cell["tokens"], logits, frames)
    93	            entry["d8_history"] = sf.run_history(model, spec, cell["tokens"])
    94	            inst = M.instances(name, 8, 2)
    95	            entry["naive_B_d8"] = sf.naive_check(model, past, spec, inst[0]["tokens"], frames, "B")
    96	            entry["naive_A_d8"] = sf.naive_check(model, past, spec, inst[0]["tokens"], frames, "A")
    97	            print(f"probe {name} d8 exact={entry['d8_canonical']['exact']} "
    98	                  f"bitwise={entry['d8_canonical']['n_logits_bitwise']}/{entry['d8_canonical']['n_cert_rows']} "
    99	                  f"frames={entry['d8_canonical']['n_frames_bitwise']} ms={entry['d8_canonical']['ms_per_step']:.1f} "
   100	                  f"history_exact={entry['d8_history'].get('exact')} "
   101	                  f"naiveB={entry['naive_B_d8']['by_depth']}", flush=True)
   102	            del past, logits, frames
   103	            fwd.torch_empty()
   104	        res["machines"][name] = entry
   105	        atomic_json(out / "probe.json", res)
   106
