   110	
   111	def counted(out, name, j, adapter2=None):
   112	    import scan_forward as fwd
   113	    model, tok, pool = load(adapter2)
   114	    host = host_record()
   115	    spec, past, rec, logits, frames = setup(model, pool, name, j)
   116	    base = {"seed": M.SEED, "host": host, "machine": name, "assign": j, "adapter2": adapter2}
   117	    atomic_json(out / "cert.json", base | {"cert": public_cert(rec)})
   118	    print(f"cert {name} assign={j} {rec['n_ok']}/{rec['n_rows']} min_margin={rec['min_margin']:.3f}", flush=True)
   119	    if not rec["pass"]:
   120	        sys.exit(3)
   121	    program = spec["program"]
   122	
   123	    def canon(tokens):
   124	        return sf.run_canonical(model, past, spec, tokens, logits, frames)
   125	
   126	    cells = []
   127	    for c in M.CELLS:
   128	        made = M.cell_input(name, c)
   129	        ref = M.run(program, made["tokens"])
   130	        got = canon(made["tokens"])
   131	        got.update({"cell": c["cell"], "ref_value": M.value_of(program, ref["q"]), "ref_max_depth": ref["max_depth"],
   132	                    "token_sha256": M.token_hash(made["tokens"])})
   133	        got["verdict_ok"] = got["exact"] and got["value"] is got["ref_value"]
   134	        cells.append(got)
   135	        atomic_json(out / "cells.json", base | {"cells": cells})
   136	        print(f"cell {c['cell']} steps={got['n_steps']} exact={got['exact']} depth={got['max_depth']} "
   137	              f"bitwise={got['n_logits_bitwise']}/{got['n_cert_rows']} frames={got['n_frames_bitwise']} "
   138	              f"ms={got['ms_per_step']:.2f}", flush=True)
   139	    malformed = []
   140	    for label, tokens in M.malformed_tokens(name).items():
   141	        got = canon(tokens)
   142	        ref = M.run(program, tokens)
   143	        got.update({"label": label, "ref_final": ref["q"]})
   144	        got["ends_in_ref_sink"] = got["exact"] and got["final_q"] == ref["q"]
   145	        malformed.append(got)
   146	    atomic_json(out / "malformed.json", base | {"malformed": malformed})
   147	    inst_rows = []
   148	    for depth in M.INSTANCE_DEPTHS:
   149	        for x in M.instances(name, depth):
   150	            got = canon(x["tokens"])
   151	            got.update({"depth": depth, "i": x["i"], "ref_value": x["value"], "token_sha256": x["token_sha256"]})
   152	            got["verdict_ok"] = got["exact"] and got["value"] is x["value"]
   153	            inst_rows.append(got)
   154	        atomic_json(out / "instances.json", base | {"instances": inst_rows})
   155	        print(f"instances d{depth} exact={sum(r['exact'] for r in inst_rows if r['depth'] == depth)}/"
   156	              f"{sum(r['depth'] == depth for r in inst_rows)}", flush=True)
   157	    if name == "bool":
   158	        bbh(out, base, model, past, spec, logits, frames)
   159	    naive = {"A": [], "B": []}
   160	    for depth in M.INSTANCE_DEPTHS:
   161	        xs = M.instances(name, depth)
   162	        use = xs if depth < 512 else xs[:2]
   163	        every = 1 if depth < 512 else 4
   164	        for variant in ("B", "A"):
   165	            for x in use:
   166	                got = sf.naive_check(model, past, spec, x["tokens"], frames, variant, every=every)
   167	                got.update({"depth": depth, "i": x["i"]})
   168	                naive[variant].append(got)
   169	        atomic_json(out / "naive.json", base | {"naive": naive})
   170	        print(f"naive d{depth} done", flush=True)
   171	    hist = {"instances": [], "cells": []}
   172	    for depth in M.INSTANCE_DEPTHS:
   173	        for x in M.instances(name, depth):
   174	            got = sf.run_history(model, spec, x["tokens"])
   175	            got.update({"depth": depth, "i": x["i"]})
   176	            hist["instances"].append(got)
   177	        atomic_json(out / "history.json", base | {"history": hist})
   178	        print(f"history d{depth} exact={sum(bool(r.get('exact')) for r in hist['instances'] if r['depth'] == depth)}"
   179	              f"/{sum(r['depth'] == depth for r in hist['instances'])}", flush=True)
   180	    for c in M.CELLS:
   181	        made = M.cell_input(name, c)
   182	        got = sf.run_history(model, spec, made["tokens"])
   183	        got.update({"cell": c["cell"]})
   184	        hist["cells"].append(got)
   185	        atomic_json(out / "history.json", base | {"history": hist})
   186	    atomic_json(out / "run.json", base | {"completed": True})
   187	    del past
   188	    fwd.torch_empty()
   189	
   190	
   191	def bbh(out, base, model, past, spec, logits, frames):
   192	    examples = json.loads(pathlib.Path(BBH_PATH).read_text())["examples"]
   193	    rows = []
   194	    for i, ex in enumerate(examples):
   195	        tokens, py_val = M.compile_bbh(ex["input"])
   196	        tokens = tokens + ["END"]
   197	        got = sf.run_canonical(model, past, spec, tokens, logits, frames)
   198	        hist = sf.run_history(model, spec, tokens)
   199	        target = ex["target"] == "True"
   200	        rows.append({"i": i, "n_tokens": len(tokens), "target": target, "py": py_val,
   201	                     "canonical_value": got["value"], "canonical_exact": got["exact"],
   202	                     "canonical_correct": got["exact"] and got["value"] is target,
   203	                     "logits_bitwise": got["n_logits_bitwise"], "cert_rows": got["n_cert_rows"],
   204	                     "frames_bitwise": got["n_frames_bitwise"], "ms_per_step": got["ms_per_step"],
   205	                     "max_depth": got["max_depth"], "history_exact": hist.get("exact"),
   206	                     "history_first_error": hist.get("first_error")})
   207	    n_ok = sum(r["canonical_correct"] for r in rows)
   208	    atomic_json(out / "bbh.json", base | {"n": len(rows), "canonical_correct": n_ok,
   209	                                          "history_exact": sum(bool(r["history_exact"]) for r in rows),
   210	                                          "rows": rows})
   211	    print(f"bbh canonical {n_ok}/{len(rows)} history {sum(bool(r['history_exact']) for r in rows)}", flush=True)
   212	
   213	
   214	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- L4

   216	
   217	def l4(out, assign_text):
   218	    import scan_forward as fwd
   219	    model, tok, pool = load()
   220	    host = host_record()
   221	    fixed = parse_assign(assign_text)
   222	    res = {"seed": M.SEED, "host": host, "machines": {}}
   223	    for name in ("dyck2", "dyck4", "bool"):
   224	        spec, past, rec, logits, frames = setup(model, pool, name, fixed[name])
   225	        entry = {"cert": cert_summary(rec), "cells": []}
   226	        print(f"l4 cert {name} {rec['n_ok']}/{rec['n_rows']} min_margin={rec['min_margin']:.3f}", flush=True)
   227	        if rec["pass"]:
   228	            for c in M.CELLS[:3]:
   229	                made = M.cell_input(name, c)
   230	                got = sf.run_canonical(model, past, spec, made["tokens"], logits, frames)
   231	                got["cell"] = c["cell"]
   232	                got["ref_value"] = M.value_of(spec["program"], M.run(spec["program"], made["tokens"])["q"])
   233	                entry["cells"].append(got)
   234	                print(f"l4 {name} {c['cell']} exact={got['exact']} ms={got['ms_per_step']:.2f}", flush=True)
   235	        res["machines"][name] = entry
   236	        atomic_json(out / "l4.json", res)
   237	        del past, logits, frames
   238	        fwd.torch_empty()

   243	THINK_TEMPLATE = "<|im_start|>user\n{q}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n"
   244	BRACKETS = {"O0": "(", "C0": ")", "O1": "[", "C1": "]", "O2": "{", "C2": "}", "O3": "<", "C3": ">"}

   246	
   247	def question(name, tokens):
   248	    body = tokens[:-1]
   249	    if name == "bool":
   250	        expr = M.tokens_to_expr(body)
   251	        return ("Evaluate the following Boolean expression (Python semantics). End your reply with a final line of the "
   252	                f"form 'Answer: True' or 'Answer: False'.\n\n{expr}")
   253	    seq = " ".join(BRACKETS[t] for t in body)
   254	    return ("Is the following sequence of brackets balanced, i.e. is every opening bracket closed by a bracket of the "
   255	            "same type in the correct nesting order, with nothing left open? End your reply with a final line of the "
   256	            f"form 'Answer: Yes' or 'Answer: No'.\n\n{seq}")
   257	
   258	
   259	def parse_answer(name, text):
   260	    if "</think>" not in text:
   261	        return None
   262	    tail = text.split("</think>")[-1]
   263	    hits = re.findall(r"Answer:\s*\**\s*(True|False|Yes|No)", tail)
   264	    if not hits:
   265	        return None
   266	    return hits[-1] in ("True", "Yes")
   267	
   268	
   269	def think(out, max_model_len=32768):
   270	    from tokenizers import Tokenizer
   271	    from huggingface_hub import snapshot_download
   272	    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
   273	    import os
   274	    path = snapshot_download("Qwen/Qwen3-4B")
   275	    tok = Tokenizer.from_file(os.path.join(path, "tokenizer.json"))
   276	    items = []
   277	    for name in ("dyck2", "dyck4", "bool"):
   278	        for depth in M.INSTANCE_DEPTHS:
   279	            for x in M.instances(name, depth):
   280	                items.append({"machine": name, "set": "instances", "depth": depth, "i": x["i"], "tokens": x["tokens"],
   281	                              "value": x["value"]})
   282	        for c in M.CELLS[:3]:
   283	            made = M.cell_input(name, c)
   284	            program = M.programs()[name]
   285	            items.append({"machine": name, "set": "cells", "cell": c["cell"], "depth": c["depth"],
   286	                          "tokens": made["tokens"], "value": M.value_of(program, M.run(program, made["tokens"])["q"])})
   287	    im_end = tok.token_to_id("<|im_end|>")
   288	    prompts, params = [], []
   289	    for i, it in enumerate(items):
   290	        q = question(it["machine"], it["tokens"])
   291	        ids = tok.encode(THINK_TEMPLATE.format(q=q), add_special_tokens=False).ids
   292	        it["prompt_tokens"] = len(ids)
   293	        prompts.append({"prompt_token_ids": ids})
   294	        params.append(SamplingParams(temperature=0.6, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, seed=i, stop_token_ids=[im_end],
   295	                                     max_tokens=max(16, max_model_len - len(ids) - 8), detokenize=False))
   296	    llm = LLM(model="Qwen/Qwen3-4B", dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=max_model_len, gpu_memory_utilization=0.9,
   297	              seed=0)
   298	    t0 = time.time()
   299	    outs = llm.generate(prompts, params)
   300	    wall = time.time() - t0
   301	    rows = []
   302	    for it, o in zip(items, outs):
   303	        ids = list(o.outputs[0].token_ids)
   304	        text = tok.decode(ids, skip_special_tokens=False)
   305	        ans = parse_answer(it["machine"], text)
   306	        rows.append({k: it[k] for k in it if k != "tokens"} | {
   307	            "n_symbols": len(it["tokens"]) - 1, "answer": ans, "correct": ans is it["value"],
   308	            "response_tokens": len(ids), "hit_max": o.outputs[0].finish_reason == "length",
   309	            "tail": text[-400:]})
   310	    atomic_json(out / "think.json", {"seed": M.SEED, "host": host_record(), "wall": wall, "max_model_len": max_model_len,
   311	                                     "sampling": {"temperature": 0.6, "top_p": 0.95, "top_k": 20}, "rows": rows})
   312	    for name in ("dyck2", "dyck4", "bool"):
   313	        for depth in M.INSTANCE_DEPTHS:
   314	            sel = [r for r in rows if r["machine"] == name and r["set"] == "instances" and r["depth"] == depth]
   315	            print(f"think {name} d{depth} {sum(r['correct'] for r in sel)}/{len(sel)}", flush=True)
   316	
   317	
   318	def main():
     1	"""
     2	Visibly pushdown machines for the stack pilot, and the inputs the certificate is about.
     3	
     4	Nothing here imports torch. The host owns the stack. The model is only asked for the next
     5	control state of a row (q, top, a). The stack operation is a pure function of that same row.
     6	
     7	Return-value transfer. A return pops the top frame and the next state is combine(child state, frame),
     8	not a restored parent state. For NOT, the two child states produce opposite results. For AND, both
     9	ANDT and ANDF do. For OR, ORF does (false or the child equals the child); ORT yields true for both
    10	children because the left operand was already true. For Dyck, a matching close in OK stays OK and the
    11	same close in BAD stays BAD, so a failed child is not rewritten to OK.
    12	
    13	Bottom-of-stack rows are returns while the top is BOT: the stack stays, the state becomes the error
    14	state. Malformed rows are every other transition the machine rejects. Halting rows are the sinks
    15	HT, HF, HE (boolean) and HA, HR (Dyck): every symbol stays there and the stack is frozen.
    16	"""
    17	
    18	from __future__ import annotations
    19	
    20	import ast
    21	import hashlib
    22	import json
    23	import pathlib
    24	import random
    25	
    26	SEED = 20261029
    27	
    28	BOOL_STATES = ("Z", "T", "F", "E", "HT", "HF", "HE")
    29	BOOL_FRAMES = ("BOT", "AND0", "ANDT", "ANDF", "OR0", "ORT", "ORF", "NOT")
    30	BOOL_ALPHABET = ("AND", "OR", "NOT", "TT", "FF", "RP", "END")
    31	BOOL_CALLS = ("AND", "OR", "NOT")
    32	BOOL_RETURNS = ("RP",)
    33	BOOL_INTERNAL = ("TT", "FF", "END")
    34	BOOL_HALT = ("HT", "HF", "HE")
    35	
    36	DYCK_STATES = ("OK", "BAD", "HA", "HR")
    37	DYCK_HALT = ("HA", "HR")
    38	
    39	
    40	def apply(stack, op):
    41	    """One stack operation. `stack` is a non-empty tuple; BOT is stack[0] at the start."""
    42	    kind = op[0]
    43	    if kind == "stay":
    44	        return stack
    45	    if kind == "rewrite":
    46	        return stack[:-1] + (op[1],)
    47	    if kind == "push":
    48	        return stack + (op[1],)
    49	    if kind == "pop":
    50	        if len(stack) < 2:
    51	            raise RuntimeError("pop on the bottom frame")
    52	        return stack[:-1]
    53	    if kind == "store_push":
    54	        return stack[:-1] + (op[1], op[2])
    55	    raise RuntimeError(f"unknown op {op}")
    56	
    57	
    58	def _blind_bool(top, a):
    59	    """Visible push, pop, or neither, into the error state. Halt entry on END."""
    60	    if a == "END":
    61	        return "HE", ("stay",)
    62	    if a in BOOL_CALLS:
    63	        frame = {"AND": "AND0", "OR": "OR0", "NOT": "NOT"}[a]
    64	        return "E", ("push", frame)
    65	    if a == "RP":
    66	        return "E", (("pop",) if top != "BOT" else ("stay",))
    67	    return "E", ("stay",)
    68	
    69	
    70	def _bool_good():
    71	    """Transitions whose target is not the error sink. Everything else is malformed or a halt entry."""
    72	    good = {}
    73	
    74	    def add(q, top, a, q2, op):
    75	        good[(q, top, a)] = (q2, op)
    76	
    77	    for top in BOOL_FRAMES:
    78	        add("Z", top, "AND", "Z", ("push", "AND0"))
    79	        add("Z", top, "OR", "Z", ("push", "OR0"))
    80	        add("Z", top, "NOT", "Z", ("push", "NOT"))
    81	    add("Z", "AND0", "TT", "Z", ("rewrite", "ANDT"))
    82	    add("Z", "AND0", "FF", "Z", ("rewrite", "ANDF"))
    83	    add("Z", "OR0", "TT", "Z", ("rewrite", "ORT"))
    84	    add("Z", "OR0", "FF", "Z", ("rewrite", "ORF"))
    85	    for top in ("ANDT", "ANDF", "ORT", "ORF", "NOT", "BOT"):
    86	        add("Z", top, "TT", "T", ("stay",))
    87	        add("Z", top, "FF", "F", ("stay",))
    88	    # A nested left operand just returned its value into the state, and the right operand is a literal.
    89	    # The literal becomes the state; the frame stores the left value. The following return combines them.
    90	    add("T", "AND0", "TT", "T", ("rewrite", "ANDT"))
    91	    add("T", "AND0", "FF", "F", ("rewrite", "ANDT"))
    92	    add("F", "AND0", "TT", "T", ("rewrite", "ANDF"))
    93	    add("F", "AND0", "FF", "F", ("rewrite", "ANDF"))
    94	    add("T", "OR0", "TT", "T", ("rewrite", "ORT"))
    95	    add("T", "OR0", "FF", "F", ("rewrite", "ORT"))
    96	    add("F", "OR0", "TT", "T", ("rewrite", "ORF"))
    97	    add("F", "OR0", "FF", "F", ("rewrite", "ORF"))
    98	    for q, stored in (("T", "ANDT"), ("F", "ANDF")):
    99	        for a, pushed in (("AND", "AND0"), ("OR", "OR0"), ("NOT", "NOT")):
   100	            add(q, "AND0", a, "Z", ("store_push", stored, pushed))
   101	    for q, stored in (("T", "ORT"), ("F", "ORF")):
   102	        for a, pushed in (("AND", "AND0"), ("OR", "OR0"), ("NOT", "NOT")):
   103	            add(q, "OR0", a, "Z", ("store_push", stored, pushed))
   104	    # Pop rows. The child state is q and the frame holds the operator and any left operand.
   105	    add("T", "NOT", "RP", "F", ("pop",))
   106	    add("F", "NOT", "RP", "T", ("pop",))
   107	    add("T", "ANDT", "RP", "T", ("pop",))
   108	    add("F", "ANDT", "RP", "F", ("pop",))
   109	    add("T", "ANDF", "RP", "F", ("pop",))
   110	    add("F", "ANDF", "RP", "F", ("pop",))
   111	    add("T", "ORT", "RP", "T", ("pop",))
   112	    add("F", "ORT", "RP", "T", ("pop",))
   113	    add("T", "ORF", "RP", "T", ("pop",))
   114	    add("F", "ORF", "RP", "F", ("pop",))
   115	    add("T", "BOT", "END", "HT", ("stay",))
   116	    add("F", "BOT", "END", "HF", ("stay",))
   117	    return good
   118	
   119	
   120	_BOOL_GOOD = _bool_good()
   121	
   122	
   123	def bool_delta(q, top, a):
   124	    if q in BOOL_HALT:
   125	        return q, ("stay",)
   126	    if q == "E":
   127	        return _blind_bool(top, a)
   128	    hit = _BOOL_GOOD.get((q, top, a))
   129	    if hit is not None:
   130	        return hit
   131	    if a == "END":
   132	        return "HE", ("stay",)
   133	    return _blind_bool(top, a)
   134	
   135	
   136	def dyck_symbols(k):
   137	    opens = tuple(f"O{i}" for i in range(k))
   138	    closes = tuple(f"C{i}" for i in range(k))
   139	    frames = ("BOT",) + tuple(f"F{i}" for i in range(k))
   140	    alphabet = opens + closes + ("END",)
   141	    return opens, closes, frames, alphabet
   142	
   143	
   144	def make_dyck_delta(k):
   145	    opens, closes, _, _ = dyck_symbols(k)
   146	    frame = {f"O{i}": f"F{i}" for i in range(k)}
   147	    close_i = {c: i for i, c in enumerate(closes)}
   148	
   149	    def delta(q, top, a):
   150	        if q in DYCK_HALT:
   151	            return q, ("stay",)
   152	        if a == "END":
   153	            if q == "OK" and top == "BOT":
   154	                return "HA", ("stay",)
   155	            return "HR", ("stay",)
   156	        if a in opens:
   157	            return ("OK" if q == "OK" else "BAD"), ("push", frame[a])
   158	        i = close_i[a]
   159	        if top == "BOT":
   160	            return "BAD", ("stay",)
   161	        if q == "OK" and top == f"F{i}":
   162	            return "OK", ("pop",)
   163	        return "BAD", ("pop",)
   164	
   165	    return delta
   166	
   167	
   168	def bool_program():
   169	    return {
   170	        "name": "bool",
   171	        "states": BOOL_STATES,
   172	        "frames": BOOL_FRAMES,
   173	        "alphabet": BOOL_ALPHABET,
   174	        "calls": BOOL_CALLS,
   175	        "returns": BOOL_RETURNS,
   176	        "internal": BOOL_INTERNAL,
   177	        "halt": BOOL_HALT,
   178	        "delta": bool_delta,
   179	        "start_q": "Z",
   180	        "start_top": "BOT",
   181	    }
   182	
   183	
   184	def dyck_program(k):
   185	    opens, closes, frames, alphabet = dyck_symbols(k)
   186	    return {
   187	        "name": f"dyck{k}",
   188	        "k": k,
   189	        "states": DYCK_STATES,
   190	        "frames": frames,
   191	        "alphabet": alphabet,
   192	        "calls": opens,
   193	        "returns": closes,
   194	        "internal": ("END",),
   195	        "halt": DYCK_HALT,
   196	        "delta": make_dyck_delta(k),
   197	        "start_q": "OK",
   198	        "start_top": "BOT",
   199	    }
   200	
   201	
   202	def programs():
   203	    return {"dyck2": dyck_program(2), "dyck4": dyck_program(4), "bool": bool_program()}
   204	
   205	
   206	def run(program, tokens):
   207	    """Reference execution. `tokens` may end with END. Returns the trace and the max frames above BOT."""
   208	    q = program["start_q"]
   209	    stack = (program["start_top"],)
   210	    trace = []
   211	    max_depth = 0
   212	    for a in tokens:
   213	        if a not in program["alphabet"]:
   214	            raise RuntimeError(f"{program['name']} has no symbol {a}")
   215	        top = stack[-1]
   216	        q2, op = program["delta"](q, top, a)
   217	        trace.append({"q": q, "top": top, "a": a, "q2": q2, "op": list(op)})
   218	        stack = apply(stack, op)
   219	        q = q2
   220	        max_depth = max(max_depth, len(stack) - 1)
   221	    return {"q": q, "stack": list(stack), "trace": trace, "max_depth": max_depth, "n": len(tokens)}
   222	
   223	
   224	def value_of(program, final_q):
   225	    """Boolean T/F, Dyck accept, or None when the machine halted in error / did not halt."""
   226	    if program["name"] == "bool":
   227	        if final_q in ("T", "HT"):
   228	            return True
   229	        if final_q in ("F", "HF"):
   230	            return False
   231	        return None
   232	    if final_q == "HA":
   233	        return True
   234	    if final_q == "HR":
   235	        return False
   236	    return None
   237	
   238	
   239	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- reachable rows
   240	
   241	
   242	def reachable(program, stack_limit=8):
   243	    """Rows witnessed by a concrete stack.
   244	
   245	    The search state is (q, top, parent). One witness stack is kept per state. Pop uses that
   246	    stack, so the grandparent it reveals is the witness's grandparent. `stack_limit` bounds
   247	    how tall a witness may grow; the pair set is recomputed at two limits by `stable_rows`.
   248	    """
   249	    delta = program["delta"]
   250	    alphabet = program["alphabet"]
   251	    start_q, start_top = program["start_q"], program["start_top"]
   252	    # parent None means the stack is exactly (BOT,)
   253	    witness = {(start_q, start_top, None): (start_top,)}
   254	    rows = {}
   255	    work = [(start_q, start_top, None)]
   256	    head = 0
   257	    while head < len(work):
   258	        q, top, parent = work[head]
   259	        head += 1
   260	        stack = witness[(q, top, parent)]
   261	        for a in alphabet:
   262	            q2, op = delta(q, top, a)
   263	            rows.setdefault((q, top, a), (q2, op))
   264	            stack2 = apply(stack, op)
   265	            if len(stack2) - 1 > stack_limit:
   266	                continue
   267	            parent2 = stack2[-2] if len(stack2) >= 2 else None
   268	            key = (q2, stack2[-1], parent2)
   269	            if key not in witness:
   270	                witness[key] = stack2

   274	
   275	def stable_rows(program, lo=6, hi=10):
   276	    """The witnessed row set once raising the stack limit stops adding (q, top) pairs."""
   277	    rows_lo, wit_lo = reachable(program, lo)
   278	    rows_hi, wit_hi = reachable(program, hi)
   279	    pairs_lo = {(q, top) for q, top, _ in wit_lo}
   280	    pairs_hi = {(q, top) for q, top, _ in wit_hi}
   281	    return {
   282	        "rows": rows_hi,
   283	        "witness": wit_hi,
   284	        "pairs_lo": pairs_lo,
   285	        "pairs_hi": pairs_hi,
   286	        "stable": pairs_lo == pairs_hi,
   287	        "lo": lo,
   288	        "hi": hi,
   289	    }
   290	
   291	
   292	def row_records(program, found=None):
   293	    """One record per witnessed row, with a class: halt, halt_entry, bottom, malformed, return, step."""
   294	    found = found if found is not None else stable_rows(program)
   295	    if not found["stable"]:
   296	        raise RuntimeError(f"{program['name']} reachable pairs grew between stack limits")
   297	    calls, returns = set(program["calls"]), set(program["returns"])
   298	    halt = set(program["halt"])
   299	    out = []
   300	    for (q, top, a), (q2, op) in sorted(found["rows"].items()):
   301	        if q in halt:
   302	            kind = "halt"
   303	        elif a == "END":
   304	            kind = "halt_entry"
   305	        elif a in returns and top == "BOT":
   306	            kind = "bottom"
   307	        elif q2 in ("E", "BAD", "HR") and not (q == "BAD" and a in returns and top != "BOT"):
   308	            kind = "malformed"
   309	        elif a in returns:
   310	            kind = "return"
   311	        elif a in calls:
   312	            kind = "call"
   313	        else:
   314	            kind = "internal"
   315	        out.append({
   316	            "q": q, "top": top, "a": a, "q2": q2, "op": list(op), "kind": kind,
   317	        })
   318	    return out
   319	
   320	
   321	def dyck_pairs_hand(k):
   322	    """Independent description of the Dyck control pairs, for the stability check."""
   323	    frames = [f"F{i}" for i in range(k)]
   324	    pairs = {("OK", "BOT"), ("BAD", "BOT"), ("HA", "BOT"), ("HR", "BOT")}
   325	    for f in frames:
   326	        pairs.add(("OK", f))
   327	        pairs.add(("BAD", f))
   328	        pairs.add(("HR", f))
   329	    return pairs

   331	
   332	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- boolean source language
   333	
   334	
   335	def emit(node):
   336	    """Prefix tokens for a Python boolean AST. `and` / `or` chains are left-associative."""
   337	    if isinstance(node, ast.Expression):
   338	        return emit(node.body)
   339	    if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and node.value is True:
   340	        return ["TT"]
   341	    if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and node.value is False:
   342	        return ["FF"]
   343	    if isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp) and isinstance(node.op, ast.Not):
   344	        return ["NOT", *emit(node.operand), "RP"]
   345	    if isinstance(node, ast.BoolOp) and isinstance(node.op, (ast.And, ast.Or)):
   346	        call = "AND" if isinstance(node.op, ast.And) else "OR"
   347	        acc = emit(node.values[0])
   348	        for value in node.values[1:]:
   349	            acc = [call, *acc, *emit(value), "RP"]
   350	        return acc
   351	    raise ValueError(f"not a boolean expression node: {type(node).__name__}")
   352	
   353	
   354	def ast_value(node):
   355	    if isinstance(node, ast.Expression):
   356	        return ast_value(node.body)
   357	    if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and isinstance(node.value, bool):
   358	        return bool(node.value)
   359	    if isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp) and isinstance(node.op, ast.Not):
   360	        return not ast_value(node.operand)
   361	    if isinstance(node, ast.BoolOp) and isinstance(node.op, ast.And):
   362	        return all(ast_value(v) for v in node.values)
   363	    if isinstance(node, ast.BoolOp) and isinstance(node.op, ast.Or):
   364	        return any(ast_value(v) for v in node.values)
   365	    raise ValueError(f"not a boolean expression node: {type(node).__name__}")
   366	
   367	
   368	def compile_expr(text):
   369	    """Tokens for a Python boolean expression. Raises ValueError if it is not one."""
   370	    tree = ast.parse(text.strip(), mode="eval")
   371	    return emit(tree), ast_value(tree)
   372	
   373	
   374	def compile_bbh(text):
   375	    body = text.strip()
   376	    if body.endswith(" is"):
   377	        body = body[:-3].strip()
   378	    return compile_expr(body)
   379	
   380	
   381	def eval_tokens(tokens):
   382	    """Value of a prefix token tree. Iterative, so a not-spine of depth 4096 does not recurse."""
   383	    stack = []
   384	    for t in tokens:
   385	        if t == "TT":
   386	            stack.append(True)
   387	        elif t == "FF":
   388	            stack.append(False)
   389	        elif t in ("AND", "OR", "NOT"):
   390	            stack.append(t)
   391	        elif t == "RP":
   392	            if len(stack) >= 2 and stack[-2] == "NOT" and isinstance(stack[-1], bool):
   393	                bit = stack.pop()
   394	                stack.pop()
   395	                stack.append(not bit)
   396	            elif (len(stack) >= 3 and stack[-3] in ("AND", "OR") and isinstance(stack[-1], bool)
   397	                  and isinstance(stack[-2], bool)):
   398	                right = stack.pop()
   399	                left = stack.pop()
   400	                op = stack.pop()
   401	                stack.append((left and right) if op == "AND" else (left or right))
   402	            else:
   403	                raise ValueError("RP does not close an operator")
   404	        else:
   405	            raise ValueError(f"bad token {t}")
   406	    if len(stack) != 1 or not isinstance(stack[0], bool):
   407	        raise ValueError("token tree did not reduce to one boolean")
   408	    return stack[0]
   409	
   410	
   411	def tokens_to_expr(tokens):
   412	    """Inverse of `emit`, so a generated token string can be checked with `ast_value`."""
   413	
   414	    def parse(i):
   415	        t = tokens[i]
   416	        if t == "TT":
   417	            return "True", i + 1
   418	        if t == "FF":
   419	            return "False", i + 1
   420	        if t == "NOT":
   421	            inner, j = parse(i + 1)
   422	            if tokens[j] != "RP":
   423	                raise ValueError("NOT without RP")
   424	            return f"(not {inner})", j + 1
   425	        if t in ("AND", "OR"):
   426	            left, j = parse(i + 1)
   427	            right, k = parse(j)
   428	            if tokens[k] != "RP":
   429	                raise ValueError("binary without RP")
   430	            op = "and" if t == "AND" else "or"
   431	            return f"({left} {op} {right})", k + 1
   432	        raise ValueError(f"bad token {t}")
   433	
   434	    expr, j = parse(0)
   435	    if j != len(tokens):
   436	        raise ValueError("trailing tokens")
   437	    return expr
   438	
   439	
   440	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- inputs
   441	
   442	
   443	def _rng(machine, cell):
   444	    return random.Random(f"{SEED}:{machine}:{cell}")
   445	
   446	
   447	def _exact_tree(rng, n, max_depth):
   448	    """A boolean token tree with exactly n tokens. n == 2 is impossible."""
   449	    if n == 2:
   450	        raise RuntimeError("no tree has 2 tokens")
   451	    if n == 1:
   452	        return ["TT" if rng.randrange(2) == 0 else "FF"]
   453	    if n == 3:
   454	        return ["NOT", *_exact_tree(rng, 1, max_depth - 1), "RP"]
   455	    if max_depth <= 1:
   456	        raise RuntimeError(f"cannot build {n} tokens at depth {max_depth}")
   457	    remain = n - 2
   458	    options = [i for i in range(1, remain) if i != 2 and remain - i != 2]
   459	    if options and max_depth >= 2:
   460	        options.sort(key=lambda i: (abs(i - remain / 2), i))
   461	        band = [i for i in options if abs(i - options[0]) <= max(1, remain // 8)]
   462	        left_n = rng.choice(band[:6] or band)
   463	        op = "AND" if rng.randrange(2) == 0 else "OR"
   464	        left = _exact_tree(rng, left_n, max_depth - 1)
   465	        right = _exact_tree(rng, remain - left_n, max_depth - 1)
   466	        return [op, *left, *right, "RP"]
   467	    return ["NOT", *_exact_tree(rng, n - 2, max_depth - 1), "RP"]
   468	
   469	
   470	def _not_spine(center, n):
   471	    return ["NOT"] * n + list(center) + ["RP"] * n
   472	
   473	
   474	def bool_tokens(cell, depth, length):
   475	    """Depth cells put a small and/or tree under a not-spine so the stack reaches exactly `depth`.
   476	
   477	    The length cell is a not-spine of 64 around a tree whose token count makes the total 20000.
   478	    A pure not-spine would never read an AND or OR row; the tree is what reads them, at that depth.
   479	    """
   480	    rng = _rng("bool", cell)
   481	    program = bool_program()
   482	    if cell == "L20000":
   483	        spine = 64
   484	        body_n = length - 2 * spine
   485	        body = _exact_tree(rng, body_n, max_depth=64)
   486	        tokens = _not_spine(body, spine)
   487	    else:
   488	        body = _exact_tree(rng, 13, max_depth=6)
   489	        got = run(program, body)["max_depth"]
   490	        if got > depth:
   491	            raise RuntimeError(f"body depth {got} exceeds requested {depth}")
   492	        tokens = _not_spine(body, depth - got)
   493	    seen = run(program, tokens)["max_depth"]
   494	    want = depth if cell != "L20000" else None
   495	    if want is not None and seen != want:
   496	        raise RuntimeError(f"{cell} reached depth {seen}, want {want}")
   497	    if cell == "L20000" and len(tokens) != length:
   498	        raise RuntimeError(f"L20000 has {len(tokens)} tokens")
   499	    return tokens
   500	
   501	
   502	def dyck_tokens(k, cell, depth, length):
   503	    """Exactly `length` brackets, maximum stack depth exactly `depth`."""
   504	    rng = _rng(f"dyck{k}", cell)
   505	    if length < 2 * depth or length % 2:
   506	        raise RuntimeError(f"dyck length {length} cannot reach depth {depth}")
   507	    seq = []
   508	    types = []
   509	    for _ in range(depth):
   510	        t = rng.randrange(k)
   511	        types.append(t)
   512	        seq.append(f"O{t}")
   513	    extra = length // 2 - depth
   514	    while extra or types:
   515	        at_cap = len(types) >= depth
   516	        at_empty = not types
   517	        if extra and not at_cap and (at_empty or rng.randrange(2) == 0):
   518	            t = rng.randrange(k)
   519	            types.append(t)
   520	            seq.append(f"O{t}")
   521	            extra -= 1
   522	        else:
   523	            seq.append(f"C{types.pop()}")
   524	    if extra or types or len(seq) != length:
   525	        raise RuntimeError("dyck generator did not hit its length")
   526	    return seq
   527	
   528	
   529	# Dyck depth cells are long enough to wander under the depth cap (at least 2*depth, even).
   530	# Dyck d4096 is the shortest word that reaches that depth. The length cell is 20000 symbols
   531	# and reaches depth 512. Boolean lengths are fixed by bool_tokens, not by this table.
   532	CELLS = (
   533	    {"cell": "d8", "depth": 8, "dyck_length": 32},
   534	    {"cell": "d64", "depth": 64, "dyck_length": 256},
   535	    {"cell": "d512", "depth": 512, "dyck_length": 2048},
   536	    {"cell": "d4096", "depth": 4096, "dyck_length": 8192},
   537	    {"cell": "L20000", "depth": 512, "dyck_length": 20000},
   538	)
   539	
   540	
   541	def _spec_for(name, spec):
   542	    if name == "bool":
   543	        length = 20000 if spec["cell"] == "L20000" else None
   544	        return spec["depth"], length
   545	    return spec["depth"], spec["dyck_length"]
   546	
   547	
   548	def malformed_tokens(name):
   549	    """Short inputs that exercise bottom, mismatch, and halt-on-error. Each ends at a halt sink."""
   550	    if name == "bool":
   551	        return {
   552	            "bottom": ["RP", "END"],
   553	            "missing": ["AND", "TT", "RP", "END"],
   554	            "extra": ["AND", "TT", "FF", "FF", "END"],
   555	        }
   556	    if name == "dyck2":
   557	        return {
   558	            "bottom": ["C0", "END"],
   559	            "mismatch": ["O0", "C1", "END"],
   560	            "propagate": ["O0", "O1", "C0", "C0", "END"],
   561	        }
   562	    return {
   563	        "bottom": ["C0", "END"],
   564	        "mismatch": ["O0", "C1", "END"],
   565	        "propagate": ["O0", "O3", "C1", "C0", "END"],
   566	    }
   567	
   568	
   569	def cell_input(name, spec):
   570	    depth, length = _spec_for(name, spec)
   571	    if name == "bool":
   572	        tokens = bool_tokens(spec["cell"], depth if spec["cell"] != "L20000" else 64, length)
   573	    elif name == "dyck2":
   574	        tokens = dyck_tokens(2, spec["cell"], depth, length)
   575	    elif name == "dyck4":
   576	        tokens = dyck_tokens(4, spec["cell"], depth, length)
   577	    else:
   578	        raise RuntimeError(name)
   579	    tokens = list(tokens) + ["END"]
   580	    return {"cell": spec["cell"], "depth_request": spec["depth"], "length_request": length, "tokens": tokens}
   581	
   582	
   583	def bool_chain(rng, depth):
   584	    """A random boolean token tree whose stack depth is exactly `depth` (>= 2).
   585	
   586	    Each level is NOT, or AND/OR with a literal sibling on a random side, so nested-left (T/F under AND0, OR0),
   587	    nested-right (store then push) and NOT rows are all read. The innermost operand is a literal.
   588	    """
   589	    program = bool_program()
   590	    levels = depth
   591	    for _ in range(64):
   592	        tokens = ["TT" if rng.randrange(2) == 0 else "FF"]
   593	        for _level in range(levels):
   594	            kind = rng.choice(("NOT", "AND", "OR"))
   595	            if kind == "NOT":
   596	                tokens = ["NOT", *tokens, "RP"]
   597	                continue
   598	            lit = "TT" if rng.randrange(2) == 0 else "FF"
   599	            if rng.randrange(2) == 0:
   600	                tokens = [kind, *tokens, lit, "RP"]
   601	            else:
   602	                tokens = [kind, lit, *tokens, "RP"]
   603	        got = run(program, tokens)["max_depth"]
   604	        if got == depth:
   605	            return tokens
   606	        levels += depth - got
   607	    raise RuntimeError(f"bool_chain could not hit depth {depth}")
   608	
   609	
   610	def dyck_instance(k, rng, depth, length, malformed):
   611	    """A random Dyck-k word of `length` symbols and max depth `depth`. A malformed word swaps the type of one
   612	    close bracket, so it is rejected with the same depth profile."""
   613	    seq = []
   614	    types = []
   615	    for _ in range(depth):
   616	        t = rng.randrange(k)
   617	        types.append(t)
   618	        seq.append(f"O{t}")
   619	    extra = length // 2 - depth
   620	    while extra or types:
   621	        if extra and len(types) < depth and (not types or rng.randrange(2) == 0):
   622	            t = rng.randrange(k)
   623	            types.append(t)
   624	            seq.append(f"O{t}")
   625	            extra -= 1
   626	        else:
   627	            seq.append(f"C{types.pop()}")
   628	    if malformed:
   629	        closes = [i for i, s in enumerate(seq) if s.startswith("C")]
   630	        i = rng.choice(closes)
   631	        t = int(seq[i][1:])
   632	        seq[i] = f"C{(t + 1 + rng.randrange(k - 1)) % k}"
   633	    return seq
   634	
   635	
   636	INSTANCE_DEPTHS = (8, 64, 512)
   637	INSTANCE_N = 8
   638	DYCK_INSTANCE_LENGTH = {8: 32, 64: 256, 512: 1536}
   639

    17	def closed_cover(program):
    18	    """Independent upper bound, closed under every possible parent exposed by pop.
    19	
    20	    Concrete witnesses provide the lower bound. Equality proves completeness;
    21	    agreement between two finite search depths alone would not prove it.
    22	    """
    23	    if program["name"] == "bool":
    24	        pairs = {(q, top) for q in ("Z", "T", "F", "E", "HE") for top in M.BOOL_FRAMES}
    25	        pairs |= {("HT", "BOT"), ("HF", "BOT")}
    26	    else:
    27	        pairs = M.dyck_pairs_hand(program["k"])
    28	    assert (program["start_q"], program["start_top"]) in pairs
    29	    checked = 0
    30	    for q, top in pairs:
    31	        # Any non-bottom symbol may have any parent. Its deeper ancestors are
    32	        # invisible to delta/apply, and cannot affect the next control pair.
    33	        stacks = [("BOT",)] if top == "BOT" else [
    34	            ("BOT", top) if parent == "BOT" else ("BOT", parent, top)
    35	            for parent in program["frames"]
    36	        ]
    37	        for stack in stacks:
    38	            for event in program["alphabet"]:
    39	                q2, op = program["delta"](q, top, event)
    40	                next_stack = M.apply(stack, op)
    41	                assert next_stack[0] == "BOT" and "BOT" not in next_stack[1:]
    42	                assert (q2, next_stack[-1]) in pairs, (q, stack, event, q2, next_stack)
    43	                checked += 1
    44	    found = M.stable_rows(program)
    45	    assert pairs == {(q, top) for q, top, _ in found["witness"]}
    46	    expected = {(q, top, a) for q, top in pairs for a in program["alphabet"]}
    47	    assert expected == set(found["rows"])
    48	    return {"pairs": len(pairs), "rows": len(expected), "closure_checks": checked,
    49	            "closed_for_arbitrary_parent": True, "all_pairs_have_concrete_witness": True}
    50

    52	def validate_results():
    53	    result = {"row_cover": {name: closed_cover(p) for name, p in M.programs().items()}, "jobs": {}}
    54	    counted_manifest = json.loads((ROOT / "manifests" / "counted.json").read_text())
    55	    l4_manifest_path = ROOT / "manifests" / "l4.json"
    56	    if l4_manifest_path.exists():
    57	        l4_manifest = json.loads(l4_manifest_path.read_text())
    58	        execution_sources = ("stack_machine.py", "stack_forward.py", "stack_job.py", "scan_forward.py", "pb_pin.py")
    59	        for name in execution_sources:
    60	            assert counted_manifest["source_hashes"][name] == l4_manifest["source_hashes"][name]
    61	        result["provenance"] = {"counted_commit": counted_manifest["commit"],
    62	                                "l4_commit": l4_manifest["commit"], "same_execution_sources": list(execution_sources)}
    63	    for name, program in M.programs().items():
    64	        directory = ROOT / "results" / f"stack-counted-{name}-h100"
    65	        if not (directory / "run.json").exists():
    66	            result["jobs"][name] = {"complete": False}
    67	            continue
    68	        read = lambda file: json.loads((directory / file).read_text())
    69	        cert = read("cert.json")["cert"]
    70	        expected = {(r["q"], r["top"], r["a"]): r["q2"] for r in M.row_records(program)}
    71	        rows = cert["rows"]
    72	        assert len(rows) == len(expected) == cert["n_ok"] == cert["n_rows"]
    73	        assert cert["pass"] and cert["assign"] == 0 and cert["min_margin"] > 0
    74	        assert {(r["q"], r["top"], r["a"]): r["q2"] for r in rows} == expected
    75	        assert all(r["ok"] and r["argmax"] == cert["ids"][r["q2"]] and r["margin"] > 0 for r in rows)
    76	        assert all(v["n"] == v["bitwise"] for v in cert["frame_slot_equal_across_rows"].values())
    77	        cells = read("cells.json")["cells"]
    78	        instances = read("instances.json")["instances"]
    79	        malformed = read("malformed.json")["malformed"]

    81	        assert len(instances) == len(M.INSTANCE_DEPTHS) * M.INSTANCE_N
    82	        assert {r["label"] for r in malformed} == set(M.malformed_tokens(name))
    83	        for c, r in zip(M.CELLS, cells):
    84	            made = M.cell_input(name, c)
    85	            ref = M.run(program, made["tokens"])
    86	            assert r["token_sha256"] == M.token_hash(made["tokens"])
    87	            assert r["max_depth"] == ref["max_depth"] and r["verdict_ok"]
    88	        expected_instances = {(x["depth"], x["i"]): x for depth in M.INSTANCE_DEPTHS
    89	                              for x in M.instances(name, depth)}
    90	        assert {(r["depth"], r["i"]) for r in instances} == set(expected_instances)
    91	        for r in instances:
    92	            assert r["token_sha256"] == expected_instances[r["depth"], r["i"]]["token_sha256"]
    93	            assert r["verdict_ok"]
    94	        for r in cells + instances + malformed:
    95	            assert r["exact"] and r["n_steps"] == r["n_tokens"] == r["n_cert_rows"]
    96	            assert r["n_steps"] == r["n_logits_bitwise"] == r["n_frames_bitwise"]
    97	            assert r["max_abs_delta"] == r["max_frame_delta"] == 0 and r["min_margin"] > 0
    98	        assert all(r["ends_in_ref_sink"] for r in malformed)
    99	        assert read("run.json")["completed"]
   100	        if name == "bool":
   101	            bbh = read("bbh.json")
   102	            assert bbh["n"] == bbh["canonical_correct"] == len(bbh["rows"]) == 250
   103	            assert {r["i"] for r in bbh["rows"]} == set(range(250))
   104	            for r in bbh["rows"]:
   105	                assert r["canonical_exact"] and r["canonical_correct"] and r["py"] is r["target"]
   106	                assert r["n_tokens"] == r["cert_rows"] == r["logits_bitwise"] == r["frames_bitwise"]
   107	        result["jobs"][name] = {"complete": True, "certificate_rows": len(rows),
   108	                                "cells": len(cells), "instances": len(instances), "malformed": len(malformed)}
   109	    l4_path = ROOT / "results" / "stack-l4" / "l4.json"

   127	    think_path = ROOT / "results" / "stack-think-h100" / "think.json"

   136	        assert all(r["correct"] == (r["answer"] is r["value"]) for r in rows)

   138	    ledger = json.loads((ROOT / "results" / "modal_ledger.json").read_text())

   142	                        "open": [name for name, j in ledger["jobs"].items() if j["status"] in ("reserved", "running")]}
   143	    return result

    28	def _ids(tok, text):
    29	    return list(tok.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False).ids)
    30	
    31	
    32	def _candidates(tok):
    33	    """Single-token names with one leading space: lower-case words, capitalised words, upper-case codes of one to
    34	    three letters, single letters. Real families use all four kinds (left, Alice, LM, H)."""
    35	    out = []
    36	    for t, i in tok.get_vocab().items():
    37	        if not t.startswith("Ġ"):
    38	            continue
    39	        w = t[1:]
    40	        if not (w.isascii() and w.isalpha()):
    41	            continue
    42	        kind = ("lower" if w.islower() and 3 <= len(w) <= 9 else
    43	                "cap" if w[0].isupper() and w[1:].islower() and 3 <= len(w) <= 9 else
    44	                "code" if w.isupper() and 1 <= len(w) <= 3 else
    45	                "letter" if len(w) == 1 else None)
    46	        if kind and w.lower() not in BLOCKED and _ids(tok, " " + w) == [i]:
    47	            out.append((w, kind))
    48	    out.sort()
    49	    random.Random(POOL_SEED).shuffle(out)
    50	    return out
    51

    53	def _fits(tok, w, wid, chosen, id_of, nl):
    54	    if _ids(tok, " " + w + "\n") != [wid, nl] or _ids(tok, "\n " + w) != [nl, wid]:
    55	        return False
    56	    for o in chosen:
    57	        oid = id_of[o]
    58	        if _ids(tok, f" {w} {o}") != [wid, oid] or _ids(tok, f" {o} {w}") != [oid, wid]:
    59	            return False
    60	    return True
    61	
    62	
    63	def name_pool(tok, n_train=240, n_test=120):
    64	    """Disjoint train and test names; every name stays one token beside every other name and beside a newline,
    65	    so a row's token ids are exactly (s, e, t, newline) and a block's are (s, e). Each split keeps the kinds'
    66	    proportions of the candidate list."""
    67	    nl = _ids(tok, "\n")
    68	    assert len(nl) == 1, "newline is not one token"
    69	    nl = nl[0]
    70	    cands = _candidates(tok)
    71	    cut = int(0.66 * len(cands))
    72	    id_of, pools = {}, {}
    73	    for split, src, n in (("train", cands[:cut], n_train), ("test", cands[cut:], n_test)):
    74	        chosen = []
    75	        for w, _kind in src:
    76	            if len(chosen) >= n:
    77	                break
    78	            wid = _ids(tok, " " + w)[0]
    79	            if w in id_of or wid in id_of.values():
    80	                continue
    81	            id_of[w] = wid
    82	            if _fits(tok, w, wid, [c for c in id_of if c != w], id_of, nl):
    83	                chosen.append(w)
    84	            else:
    85	                del id_of[w]
    86	        pools[split] = chosen
    87	    # Family alphabets the clock will walk by id: bare digits (Qwen splits digits, so " 1" is two tokens), single
    88	    # letters with a space, brackets. They are names by id only (no text round trip), and only in training.
    89	    for name, text in ([(d, d) for d in "0123456789"] + [(c, " " + c) for c in "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"]
    90	                       + [(b, b) for b in "()[]"]):
    91	        ids = _ids(tok, text)
    92	        if len(ids) == 1 and ids[0] not in id_of.values() and name not in id_of:
    93	            id_of[name] = ids[0]
    94	            pools["train"].append(name)
    95	    kinds = dict(cands)
    96	    return {"train": pools["train"], "test": pools["test"], "id_of": id_of, "nl": nl,
    97	            "kinds": {s: {k: sum(kinds.get(w) == k for w in pools[s]) for k in ("lower", "cap", "code", "letter")}
    98	                      | {"extra": sum(w not in kinds for w in pools[s])} for s in ("train", "test")}}
    99

   209	def prefill(model, pi_ids):
   210	    import torch
   211	    ids = torch.tensor([pi_ids], device=model.device)
   212	    pos = torch.arange(len(pi_ids), device=model.device)[None]
   213	    with torch.no_grad():
   214	        return model(input_ids=ids, position_ids=pos, use_cache=True).past_key_values

   217	def _clone(past):
   218	    import copy
   219	    import torch
   220	    if hasattr(past, "layers"):
   221	        new = copy.copy(past)
   222	        new.layers = []
   223	        for layer in past.layers:
   224	            c = copy.copy(layer)
   225	            for attr in ("keys", "values"):
   226	                v = getattr(c, attr, None)
   227	                if torch.is_tensor(v):
   228	                    setattr(c, attr, v.clone())
   229	            new.layers.append(c)
   230	        return new
   231	    if hasattr(past, "key_cache"):
   232	        new = copy.copy(past)
   233	        new.key_cache = [t.clone() for t in past.key_cache]
   234	        new.value_cache = [t.clone() for t in past.value_cache]
   235	        return new
   236	    return tuple(tuple(t.clone() for t in layer) for layer in past)
   237	
   238	
   239	def tick(model, past, P, s_id, e_id):
   240	    """One tick: the block [s][e] at positions P, P+1 over a copy of Π's cache. Returns the full-vocabulary
   241	    logits at the event position (the copy is discarded, so Π's cache is never written)."""
   242	    import torch
   243	    pos = torch.tensor([[P, P + 1]], device=model.device)
   244	    with torch.no_grad():
   245	        out = model(input_ids=torch.tensor([[s_id, e_id]], device=model.device), position_ids=pos,
   246	                    cache_position=pos[0], past_key_values=_clone(past), use_cache=True)
   247	    return out.logits[0, -1]

    52	def load_merged(model_name=MODEL_NAME, adapter=ADAPTER):
    53	    """Merge the adapter into a new directory and load it. A warm container must not reuse a previous merge."""
    54	    import torch
    55	    from pb_eval import merge_adapter
    56	    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM
    57	    if os.path.exists(MERGE_DIR):
    58	        shutil.rmtree(MERGE_DIR)
    59	    merge_adapter(model_name, adapter, MERGE_DIR)
    60	    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
    61	        MERGE_DIR, dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation="sdpa").cuda().eval()
    62	    return model

    71	    torch.cuda.synchronize()

   482	def prefill(model, pi_ids):
   483	    import torch
   484	    import pb_pin
   485	    device = _device(model)
   486	    ids = torch.tensor([pi_ids], device=device)
   487	    pos = torch.arange(len(pi_ids), device=device)[None]
   488	    with torch.inference_mode():
   489	        past = model(input_ids=ids, position_ids=pos, use_cache=True).past_key_values
   490	    if past.get_seq_length() != len(pi_ids):
   491	        raise RuntimeError(f"prefill length {past.get_seq_length()} != {len(pi_ids)}")
   492	    return past

   571	def single_lookup(model, past, P, block):
   572	    """The pinned tick: one block, the model's own causal mask, positions restarted. Returns the logit vector."""
   573	    import torch
   574	    import pb_pin
   575	    device = _device(model)
   576	    ids = torch.tensor([list(block)], device=device)
   577	    pos = torch.tensor([list(range(P, P + len(block)))], device=device)
   578	    cache = pb_pin._clone(past)
   579	    with torch.inference_mode():
   580	        out = model(input_ids=ids, position_ids=pos, cache_position=pos[0], past_key_values=cache, use_cache=True)
   581	    logit = out.logits[0, -1].clone()
   582	    del out, cache
   583	    return logit

    23	
    24	
    25	def random_table(rng, names):
    26	    n_q, n_g, n_a = rng.randint(3, 8), rng.randint(3, 9), rng.randint(4, 9)
    27	    pick = rng.sample(names, n_q + n_g + n_a)
    28	    states, frames, events = pick[:n_q], pick[n_q:n_q + n_g], pick[n_q + n_g:]
    29	    keys = [(g, q, a) for q in states for g in frames for a in events]
    30	    rng.shuffle(keys)
    31	    keys = keys[:min(len(keys), rng.randint(40, 300))]
    32	    sticky = rng.random() * 0.5
    33	    rows = []
    34	    for g, q, a in keys:
    35	        q2 = q if rng.random() < sticky else rng.choice(states)
    36	        rows.append((g, q, a, q2))
    37	    if rng.random() < 0.5:
    38	        rows.sort(key=lambda r: (r[1], r[0], r[2]))
    39	    return rows
    40	
    41	
    42	def example(rng, pool, blocks):
    43	    rows = random_table(rng, pool["train"])
    44	    io, nl = pool["id_of"], pool["nl"]
    45	    pi = []
    46	    for g, q, a, q2 in rows:
    47	        pi += [io[g], io[q], io[a], io[q2], nl]
    48	    pick = [rng.choice(rows) for _ in range(blocks)]
    49	    return {"pi": pi, "blocks": [(io[g], io[q], io[a]) for g, q, a, _ in pick], "labels": [io[q2] for *_, q2 in pick]}
    50	
    51	
    52	def packed_batch(exs, pad_id, block_len=3):
    53	    import torch
    54	    seqs = []
    55	    for ex in exs:
    56	        P = len(ex["pi"])
    57	        ids, pos, owner, lab_pos = list(ex["pi"]), list(range(P)), [-1] * P, []
    58	        for b, blk in enumerate(ex["blocks"]):
    59	            ids += list(blk)
    60	            pos += list(range(P, P + block_len))
    61	            owner += [b] * block_len
    62	            lab_pos.append(len(ids) - 1)
    63	        seqs.append((ids, pos, owner, lab_pos))
    64	    L = max(len(s[0]) for s in seqs)
    65	    B = len(seqs)
    66	    ids_t = torch.full((B, L), pad_id, dtype=torch.long)
    67	    pos_t = torch.zeros((B, L), dtype=torch.long)
    68	    allow = torch.zeros((B, 1, L, L), dtype=torch.bool)
    69	    rows, cols, labels = [], [], []
    70	    for i, ((sid, spos, owner, lab_pos), ex) in enumerate(zip(seqs, exs)):
    71	        n = len(sid)
    72	        ids_t[i, :n] = torch.tensor(sid)
    73	        pos_t[i, :n] = torch.tensor(spos)
    74	        own = torch.tensor(owner)
    75	        q = torch.arange(n)
    76	        causal = q[:, None] >= q[None, :]
    77	        same = own[:, None] == own[None, :]
    78	        to_pi = (own[None, :] < 0).expand(n, n)
    79	        allow[i, 0, :n, :n] = causal & (same | to_pi)
    80	        if n < L:
    81	            idx = torch.arange(n, L)
    82	            allow[i, 0, idx, idx] = True
    83	        for p, lab in zip(lab_pos, ex["labels"]):
    84	            rows.append(i)
    85	            cols.append(p)
    86	            labels.append(lab)
    87	    return ids_t, pos_t, allow, torch.tensor(rows), torch.tensor(cols), torch.tensor(labels)
    88	
    89	
    90	def main():
    91	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    92	    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=300)
    93	    ap.add_argument("--rank", type=int, default=16)
    94	    ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=1e-4)
    95	    ap.add_argument("--warmup", type=int, default=20)
    96	    ap.add_argument("--tables", type=int, default=8)
    97	    ap.add_argument("--micro", type=int, default=2)
    98	    ap.add_argument("--blocks", type=int, default=48)
    99	    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=M.SEED)
   100	    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
   101	    a = ap.parse_args()
   102	    if a.steps > 300:
   103	        raise SystemExit("the brief allows at most 300 LoRA steps")
   104	    import torch
   105	    import pb_pin
   106	    import scan_forward as fwd
   107	    from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model
   108	    out = pathlib.Path(a.out)
   109	    tok = fwd.load_tokenizer()
   110	    pool = pb_pin.name_pool(tok)
   111	    model = fwd.load_merged()
   112	    torch.manual_seed(a.seed)
   113	    model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False})
   114	    model.enable_input_require_grads()
   115	    model = get_peft_model(model, LoraConfig(r=a.rank, lora_alpha=2 * a.rank, lora_dropout=0.0, bias="none",
   116	                                             task_type="CAUSAL_LM", target_modules=LORA_TARGETS))
   117	    trainable = [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
   118	    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(trainable, lr=a.lr, betas=(0.9, 0.99), weight_decay=0.0)
   119	    core = model.base_model.model
   120	    rng = random.Random(a.seed)
   121	    pad = tok.token_to_id("<|im_end|>")
   122	    log = []
   123	    t0 = time.time()
   124	    model.train()
   125	    for step in range(a.steps):
   126	        lr = a.lr * (step + 1) / a.warmup if step < a.warmup else \
   127	            a.lr * (0.1 + 0.9 * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * (step - a.warmup) / max(1, a.steps - a.warmup))))
   128	        for g in opt.param_groups:
   129	            g["lr"] = lr
   130	        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
   131	        exs = sorted((example(rng, pool, a.blocks) for _ in range(a.tables)), key=lambda e: len(e["pi"]))
   132	        n = a.tables * a.blocks
   133	        loss_sum, ok = 0.0, 0
   134	        for k in range(0, len(exs), a.micro):
   135	            ids, pos, allow, r, c, lab = packed_batch(exs[k:k + a.micro], pad)
   136	            hidden = core.model(input_ids=ids.cuda(), position_ids=pos.cuda(), attention_mask=allow.cuda()).last_hidden_state
   137	            logits = core.lm_head(hidden[r.cuda(), c.cuda()]).float()
   138	            loss = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(logits, lab.cuda(), reduction="sum")
   139	            (loss / n).backward()
   140	            loss_sum += loss.item()
   141	            ok += int((logits.argmax(-1) == lab.cuda()).sum())
   142	            del hidden, logits, loss
   143	        gn = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(trainable, 1.0)
   144	        opt.step()
   145	        if step % 10 == 0 or step == a.steps - 1:
   146	            rec = {"step": step, "loss": loss_sum / n, "acc": ok / n, "lr": lr, "grad_norm": float(gn),
   147	                   "elapsed": time.time() - t0}
   148	            log.append(rec)
   149	            print(json.dumps(rec), flush=True)
   150	    model.save_pretrained(str(out / "adapter2"))
   151	    atomic_json(out / "train.json", {"args": vars(a), "log": log, "host": host_record(), "seconds": time.time() - t0})
   152	    model.eval()
   153	    merged = model.merge_and_unload().eval()
   154	    del opt
   155	    torch.cuda.empty_cache()

    31	    if isinstance(x, float):

    40	def canonical_steps(m):
    41	    """Every canonical step the counted job ran: cells, malformed, instances, and BBH for the evaluator."""
    42	    job = counted_job(m)
    43	    runs = []
    44	    for fname, key in (("cells.json", "cells"), ("malformed.json", "malformed"), ("instances.json", "instances")):
    45	        d = load(job, fname)
    46	        if d:
    47	            runs += d[key]
    48	    tot = {"steps": 0, "cert": 0, "logit_bit": 0, "frame_bit": 0, "max_delta": 0.0, "max_frame_delta": 0.0,
    49	           "min_margin": None, "depth": 0}
    50	    for r in runs:
    51	        tot["steps"] += r["n_steps"]
    52	        tot["cert"] += r["n_cert_rows"]
    53	        tot["logit_bit"] += r["n_logits_bitwise"]
    54	        tot["frame_bit"] += r["n_frames_bitwise"]
    55	        tot["max_delta"] = max(tot["max_delta"], r["max_abs_delta"])
    56	        tot["max_frame_delta"] = max(tot["max_frame_delta"], r.get("max_frame_delta", 0.0))
    57	        if r["min_margin"] is not None:
    58	            tot["min_margin"] = r["min_margin"] if tot["min_margin"] is None else min(tot["min_margin"], r["min_margin"])
    59	        tot["depth"] = max(tot["depth"], r["max_depth"])
    60	    b = load(job, "bbh.json")
    61	    if b:
    62	        for r in b["rows"]:
    63	            tot["steps"] += r["n_tokens"]
    64	            tot["cert"] += r["cert_rows"]
    65	            tot["logit_bit"] += r["logits_bitwise"]
    66	            tot["frame_bit"] += r["frames_bitwise"]
    67	    return tot

    12	        for name, n in (("dyck2", 50), ("dyck4", 144), ("bool", 294)):
    13	            self.assertEqual(closed_cover(M.programs()[name])["rows"], n)

    15	    def test_missing_row_is_rejected(self):
    16	        program = M.bool_program()
    17	        found = M.stable_rows(program)
    18	        found["rows"].pop(("F", "NOT", "RP"))
    19	        with patch.object(M, "stable_rows", return_value=found), self.assertRaises(AssertionError):
    20	            closed_cover(program)
    21

    27	            closed_cover(program)

    23	def host_record():
    24	    """CPU model, AVX-512F flag and GPU name of the machine this runs on. Goes into every record."""
    25	    info = {"gpu": None, "gpu_count": 0, "cpu": None, "cpu_vendor_family_model": None, "avx512f": None,
    26	            "platform": platform.platform(), "hostname_hash": sha256_bytes(platform.node().encode())[:12]}
    27	    try:
    28	        import torch
    29	        if torch.cuda.is_available():
    30	            info["gpu"] = torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)
    31	            info["gpu_count"] = torch.cuda.device_count()
    32	    except Exception:
    33	        pass
    34	    if info["gpu"] is None:
    35	        try:
    36	            out = subprocess.check_output(["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=name", "--format=csv,noheader"], text=True, timeout=10)
    37	            names = [x.strip() for x in out.splitlines() if x.strip()]
    38	            if names:
    39	                info["gpu"], info["gpu_count"] = names[0], len(names)
    40	        except Exception:
    41	            info["gpu"] = "none"
    42	    try:
    43	        text = pathlib.Path("/proc/cpuinfo").read_text()
    44	        fields = {}
    45	        for line in text.splitlines():
    46	            if ":" in line:
    47	                key, val = line.split(":", 1)
    48	                fields.setdefault(key.strip().lower(), val.strip())
    49	        info["cpu"] = fields.get("model name")
    50	        info["cpu_vendor_family_model"] = " ".join(
    51	            str(fields.get(k)) for k in ("vendor_id", "cpu family", "model", "stepping"))
    52	        info["avx512f"] = "avx512f" in fields.get("flags", "").split()
    53	        info["cpu_count"] = os.cpu_count()
    54	    except OSError:
    55	        try:
    56	            info["cpu"] = subprocess.check_output(["sysctl", "-n", "machdep.cpu.brand_string"], text=True, timeout=10).strip()
    57	        except Exception:
    58	            info["cpu"] = platform.processor() or None
    59	        info["avx512f"] = False
    60	        info["cpu_count"] = os.cpu_count()
    61	    return info

     1	"""
     2	Modal launcher for the stack pilot (copied from scan_modal.py). Own app `polyformer-stack`, own volume
     3	`polyformer-stack-results`.
     4	The arith volume is mounted read-only at /arvol. Importing never launches work.
     5	
     6	    uv run --no-project --with modal modal deploy research/stack/stack_modal.py
     7	    uv run --no-project --with modal python research/stack/stack_modal.py plan --name <name> --jobs <jobs.json>
     8	    uv run --no-project --with modal python research/stack/stack_modal.py spawn --manifest <m.json>
     9	    uv run --no-project --with modal python research/stack/stack_modal.py collect --manifest <m.json>
    10	    uv run --no-project --with modal python research/stack/stack_modal.py status
    11	
    12	A job is {"id", "script", "argv", "gpu": "H100" | "L4" | "none", "max_seconds"}. The script runs with `--out /vol/<id>`.
    13	The image matches closeout's packages and model snapshots so the heavy layers can be reused. The source hash is part
    14	of the image, and the call also ships the sources: the job refuses to run if they disagree.
    15	"""
    16	
    17	from __future__ import annotations
    18	
    19	import argparse
    20	import contextlib
    21	import datetime as dt
    22	import fcntl
    23	import fnmatch
    24	import hashlib
    25	import json
    26	import os
    27	import pathlib
    28	import shutil
    29	import signal
    30	import subprocess
    31	import sys
    32	import time
    33	
    34	ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    35	PB_ROOT = ROOT.parent / "promptbox"
    36	AR_ROOT = ROOT.parent / "arith"
    37	SC_ROOT = ROOT.parent / "scan"
    38	SOURCE_DIRS = (ROOT, SC_ROOT, AR_ROOT, PB_ROOT)
    39	
    40	
    41	def stamp():
    42	    return dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat()
    43	
    44	
    45	def sha256_bytes(data):
    46	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    47	
    48	
    49	def atomic_json(path, value):
    50	    path = pathlib.Path(path)
    51	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    52	    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + f".{os.getpid()}.tmp")
    53	    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str))
    54	    os.replace(tmp, path)
    55	
    56	
    57	MANIFESTS = ROOT / "manifests"
    58	RESULTS = ROOT / "results"
    59	LEDGER = RESULTS / "modal_ledger.json"
    60	LOCK = RESULTS / "modal_ledger.lock"
    61	CAP_USD = 20.0
    62	APP_NAME, VOLUME_NAME = "polyformer-stack", "polyformer-stack-results"
    63	AR_VOLUME_NAME = "polyformer-arith-results"
    64	GPU_RATE = {"H100": 0.001097, "L4": 0.000222, "none": 0.0}
    65	CPU_RATE, RAM_RATE, CPU_MAX, RAM_GIB_MAX = 0.0000131, 0.00000222, 16, 64
    66	STARTUP_SECONDS, WRAPPER_SECONDS = 150, 30
    67	MODELS = ("Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B", "Qwen/Qwen3-4B")
    68	BBH_COMMIT = "9ee07bd481feebf959a6b59d61ea57bdcf30964d"
    69	SOURCE_GLOB = "*.py"
    70	FN = {"H100": "execute_gpu", "L4": "execute_l4", "none": "execute_cpu"}
    71	
    72	
    73	def local_files():
    74	    files = [(p.name, p) for d in SOURCE_DIRS for p in sorted(d.glob(SOURCE_GLOB))]
    75	    if len({n for n, _ in files}) != len(files):
    76	        raise ValueError("a file name occurs in more than one source directory")
    77	    return files
    78	
    79	
    80	def sources(commit=None):
    81	    if commit is None:
    82	        return {n: sha256_bytes(p.read_bytes()) for n, p in local_files()}
    83	    out = {}
    84	    for d in SOURCE_DIRS:
    85	        names = subprocess.check_output(["git", "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", commit, "--", "."], cwd=d, text=True)
    86	        for n in names.split():
    87	            if fnmatch.fnmatch(n, SOURCE_GLOB) and "/" not in n:
    88	                out[n] = sha256_bytes(subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", f"{commit}:./{n}"], cwd=d))
    89	    return out
    90

L4 stores `"n_steps": 24` and `"seconds": 1.545911450999995` for its first cell at `results/stack-l4/l4.json:51–54`.

Additional recomputations also match:

- Naive **B/A errors**: Dyck-2 **19/3090, 227/3090**; Dyck-4 **294/3090, 67/3090**; Boolean **132/2270, 77/2270**. Nonbottom equality matches: **0/6104, 0/6098, 0/4472**. Every control prediction was correct. Raw buckets explicitly store `"naive_ok"`, `"control_ok"` and `"n"` (`results/stack-counted-dyck2-h100/naive.json:20–28`, corresponding files for the other machines); these reproduce `STACK.md:359–362`.
- History d8/d64/d512: **8/8,8/8,7/8**; **8/8,8/8,8/8**; **8/8,8/8,8/8**. Thinking: **5/8,0/8,0/8**; **4/8,3/8,4/8**; **8/8,2/8,6/8**. These reproduce `STACK.md:324–336`; thinking records start at `"rows": [` (`results/stack-think-h100/think.json:13`).
- Historical BBH: **248/250 = 99.2%**, **244/250 = 97.6%**, **55/250 = 22.0%**. I filtered individual records by `"bbh|boolean_expressions|official"` in `../promptbox/results/base-think-bbh/base-think-bbh_bbh_official.rescored.json:29810`, `../promptbox/results/base-answer-3/base-answer-3_bbh_official.json:28053`, and `../closeout/results/co-r2-s1/co-r2-s1_bbh_official.json:54233`.
- Spend sums to **$11.760997**, matching **$11.7610**; the ledger calls this an `"upper-rate estimate … not an invoice"` (`results/modal_ledger.json:11`; report total `STACK.md:387`).

2. **Canonical history independence and closed cover — OK.** The actual call is:

> `forward(model, cache, [top_id, q_id, a_id], [P, P + 1, P + 2], P)`  
> `stack_forward.py:112`

The preceding line clones Π. I located and read the shared helper: it clones every layer’s `"keys"` and `"values"` (`../promptbox/pb_pin.py:217–235`). No stack depth or previous-step cache enters this call.

The closure check considers every parent exposed by popping, asserts BOT preservation and successor membership, then requires exact equality with the witnessed row set (`stack_validate.py:33–47`: `"assert expected == set(found[\"rows\"])"`). Independently enumerating those transitions reproduced **110 / 576 / 2,009** closure cases. Certificate rows include bottom, malformed, halt-entry and absorbing-halt transitions; classification is explicit at `stack_machine.py:300–314`. The arbitrary-depth conclusion remains conditional on deterministic inference, as the report states at `STACK.md:375`.

3. **Correct tensors — OK; literal bitwise claim — BUG, low severity.** Logits come from the event’s full vocabulary (`stack_forward.py:102`: `"return out.logits[0, -1]"`), indexed against the actual `(top,q,a)` certificate row (`:206–210`). Frame extraction includes K and V at slot P across every layer (`:60`).

However, the checks are:

> `if torch.equal(lg, ref):`  
> `if torch.equal(fr, frames[top]):`  
> `stack_forward.py:210,214`

`torch.equal` tests numerical equality, not byte identity; signed zeros can compare equal. **Fix:** describe the existing result as exact numerical equality, or compare contiguous byte views and retain tensor hashes. **Effect:** no demonstrated change to any answer, margin or numerical-equality count; the literal byte-equality count is unknown. Saved certificate records contain scalar summaries rather than the full tensors (`stack_forward.py:147–150,171–177`).

4. **Leakage and host/model division — DESIGN FLAW for interpreting this as independent reasoning; OK for supplied-table execution.** Every queried transition’s answer is deliberately present in Π:

> `pi += [ids[r["top"]], ids[r["q"]], ids[r["a"]], ids[r["q2"]], nl]`  
> `stack_forward.py:43`

Thus **488/488 certifies retrieval/execution of supplied answers**, not discovery of transition rules.

The host parses BBH syntax, emits the program **and separately computes its value** (`stack_machine.py:370–371`: `"return emit(tree), ast_value(tree)"`). That value and the official label do not enter the model call: inference precedes `"target = ex[\"target\"] == \"True\""` (`stack_job.py:195–202`).

The host also computes the reference transition and stack operation, checks the model output, and applies push/pop/rewrite/store operations (`stack_forward.py:222–236`; `stack_machine.py:40–54`). **The model’s contribution is only full-vocabulary next-control-state selection.** No erroneous state is silently repaired on the counted path: mismatch breaks execution at `stack_forward.py:224–230`.

No additional STACK training occurred: records say `"adapter2": null`, `"assign": 0` (`results/stack-counted-bool-h100/cert.json:2–3`). The unused fallback draws from `pool["train"]` (`stack_lora.py:43`), disjoint from test names by `../promptbox/pb_pin.py:73–94`. This does not establish absence of contamination in the pre-existing model.

5. **Frozen predictions and verdict rules — BUG for L4; otherwise consistent with these records.** The registered section is unchanged from commit `9aaeaf21`. Certificate acceptance implements `"arg == target and margin > 0"` (`stack_forward.py:145`); counted verdicts require exact execution and the correct final value (`stack_job.py:133,152,202`). Reported misses—Boolean zero-shot failure, B≤A on two machines, history degradation, and several thinking thresholds—are correctly retained (`STACK.md:357–366`).

The timing registration says:

> “mean over the counted cells’ steps” — `STACK.md:181`

But L4 executes:

> `for c in M.CELLS[:3]:` — `stack_job.py:228`

The report nevertheless marks its prediction as holding (`stack_report.py:400–402`). **Fix:** mark the full-workload L4 prediction untested, or run the missing six cells. **Effect:** **63.97 ms remains correct for its subset**; the registered 15-cell L4 mean is unknown.

6. **Comparator fairness and scoring — DESIGN FLAW for a controlled capability comparison; scoring OK.** Canonical/history use the same supplied table and model, but intentionally different cache/position policies. History stops on its first wrong state (`stack_forward.py:274–283`), so its score is correctly an exact-trace score.

Thinking instead uses the **base** model, natural-language inputs, stochastic sampling and a 32,768-token context (`stack_job.py:247–256,294–297`); canonical uses merged Savant r3 (`../scan/scan_forward.py:18–19,52–61`). Training, representation, compute budget and scoring are consequently unmatched. Rank, learning-rate and checkpoint-selection comparisons are inapplicable to the untrained STACK arms; the unused fallback has one loss from the outset and saves only after training (`stack_lora.py:138–150`).

Reparsing all **81** stored tails reproduced the scores; all **25** null answers were budget-exhausted responses. The parser explicitly requires `"</think>"` and an `"Answer:"` match (`stack_job.py:259–266`).

Naive A also changes query positions, so it does not isolate stored-KV effects alone (`stack_forward.py:324–326`). Its trajectory is reference-driven (`:360`: `"q = want_q2"`), and deep sampling uses only two instances, every fourth step (`stack_job.py:162–163`). These are sampled local errors, not autonomous-run failure rates.

7. **Figures/captions — OK for the tables present.** There are no plotted figures in this report. I independently reproduced the frame-depth and naive-table entries. The caption correctly says “Each row aggregates sampled steps” (`STACK.md:249`); code sums counts and takes maxima for delta columns (`stack_report.py:194–197`). Dyck-4’s imperfect depth-zero control is disclosed (`STACK.md:284`), preventing attribution of every nonzero difference solely to history.

**BUGs by severity:** medium—L4 prediction declared passed on an incomplete workload; low—numerical equality labeled literal bitwise equality. Neither changes recorded accuracy.

**Headline claims that survive:**

- **488/488:** survives as zero-additional-training certification of supplied-table execution.
- **135,078 steps:** survives as exact numerical equality; literal byte equality remains unverified.
- **BBH 250/250:** survives for the host-compiled, host-stack pipeline.
- **Naive failures:** survives for the sampled local comparisons, with positional and numerical-control qualifications.
- **24/64 ms:** survives for the respective measured workloads; full registered L4 timing remains open.
